1→"""Language registry: plugin registration and language resolution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import inspect
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import TypeVar
     9→
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework import (
    11→    discovery,
    12→    registry_state,
    13→    resolution,
    14→    runtime,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    17→from desloppify.languages._framework.contract_validation import validate_lang_contract
    18→from desloppify.languages._framework.policy import REQUIRED_DIRS, REQUIRED_FILES
    19→from desloppify.languages._framework.resolution import (
    20→    auto_detect_lang,
    21→    available_langs,
    22→    get_lang,
    23→    make_lang_config,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.languages._framework.structure_validation import validate_lang_structure
    26→
    27→T = TypeVar("T")
    28→
    29→
    30→def register_lang(name: str) -> Callable[[T], T]:
    31→    """Decorator to register a language config class.
    32→
    33→    Validates structure, instantiates the class, validates the contract,
    34→    and stores the *instance* in the registry.
    35→    """
    36→
    37→    def decorator(cls: T) -> T:
    38→        module = inspect.getmodule(cls)
    39→        if module and hasattr(module, "__file__"):
    40→            validate_lang_structure(Path(module.__file__).parent, name)
    41→        if isinstance(cls, type) and issubclass(cls, LangConfig):
    42→            cfg = make_lang_config(name, cls)  # instantiate + validate
    43→            registry_state.register(name, cfg)  # store instance
    44→        else:
    45→            registry_state.register(name, cls)  # test doubles
    46→        return cls
    47→
    48→    return decorator
    49→
    50→
    51→def register_generic_lang(name: str, cfg: LangConfig) -> None:
    52→    """Register a pre-built language plugin instance (no package structure required)."""
    53→    validate_lang_contract(name, cfg)
    54→    registry_state.register(name, cfg)
    55→
    56→
    57→def reload_lang_plugins() -> list[str]:
    58→    """Force plugin rediscovery and return refreshed language names."""
    59→    discovery.load_all(force_reload=True)
    60→    return sorted(registry_state.all_keys())
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = [
    64→    "REQUIRED_FILES",
    65→    "REQUIRED_DIRS",
    66→    "register_lang",
    67→    "register_generic_lang",
    68→    "reload_lang_plugins",
    69→    "get_lang",
    70→    "available_langs",
    71→    "auto_detect_lang",
    72→    "make_lang_config",
    73→    "validate_lang_structure",
    74→    "validate_lang_contract",
    75→    "discovery",
    76→    "registry_state",
    77→    "resolution",
    78→    "runtime",
    79→]
    80→
```

1→# Languages
     2→
     3→Desloppify supports 28 languages through a plugin system with two tiers: **full plugins** (6) with hand-written detectors and subjective review, and **generic plugins** (22) that wrap external linters and optionally use tree-sitter for AST analysis.
     4→
     5→## Full Plugins
     6→
     7→These have custom detectors, language-specific smell analysis, subjective review dimensions, auto-fixers, and deep scoring. They're multi-module packages with the full plugin contract.
     8→
     9→| Language | Path | Key capabilities |
    10→|----------|------|-----------------|
    11→| **Python** | `python/` | AST smell detection, ruff/bandit adapters, import-linter, unused detection, security, auto-fix |
    12→| **TypeScript** | `typescript/` | React-aware detectors, knip adapter, 7 auto-fixers, props/exports/concerns analysis |
    13→| **C#/.NET** | `csharp/` | Structural + coupling + security, dotnet-based dep analysis |
    14→| **Dart** | `dart/` | Flutter-aware, pubspec integration, test coverage mapping |
    15→| **GDScript** | `gdscript/` | Godot scene-aware, tree-sitter phases, shared framework helpers |
    16→| **Go** | `go/` | golangci-lint + go vet adapters, regex function extraction, test coverage mapping |
    17→
    18→Example: `python/` — see `python/__init__.py` for the full `@register_lang()` config with 15+ detector phases, custom extractors, security hooks, and review guidance.
    19→
    20→## Generic Plugins
    21→
    22→These are single-file plugins (~20-40 lines) that call `generic_lang()` with external tool specs. When `tree-sitter-language-pack` is installed, they also get AST-powered analysis for free.
    23→
    24→| Language | Path | Tools | Tree-sitter |
    25→|----------|------|-------|-------------|
    26→| Rust | `rust/` | cargo clippy, cargo check | functions, imports, structs |
    27→| Ruby | `ruby/` | rubocop | functions, methods, classes, imports |
    28→| Java | `java/` | checkstyle | functions, constructors, classes, imports |
    29→| Kotlin | `kotlin/` | ktlint, detekt | functions, classes, imports |
    30→| Swift | `swift/` | swiftlint | functions, classes |
    31→| PHP | `php/` | phpstan | functions, methods, classes, imports |
    32→| Scala | `scala/` | scalac | functions, classes, imports |
    33→| Elixir | `elixir/` | credo | functions, imports |
    34→| Haskell | `haskell/` | hlint | functions, imports |
    35→| JavaScript | `javascript/` | eslint | functions, methods, classes, imports |
    36→| C/C++ | `cxx/` | cppcheck | functions, classes, includes |
    37→| Bash | `bash/` | shellcheck | functions, source imports |
    38→| Lua | `lua/` | luacheck | functions, imports |
    39→| Perl | `perl/` | perlcritic | subroutines, imports |
    40→| Clojure | `clojure/` | clj-kondo | functions |
    41→| Zig | `zig/` | zig build | functions, imports |
    42→| Nim | `nim/` | nim check | functions |
    43→| PowerShell | `powershell/` | PSScriptAnalyzer | functions |
    44→| R | `r/` | — | functions, imports |
    45→| Erlang | `erlang/` | dialyzer | functions, imports |
    46→| OCaml | `ocaml/` | ocaml compiler | functions, modules, imports |
    47→| F# | `fsharp/` | dotnet build | functions, imports |
    48→
    49→Example: `rust/__init__.py` — wraps cargo clippy + cargo check, adds Rust-specific zone rules and test coverage hooks.
    50→
    51→## What Each Tier Gets
    52→
    53→### Every plugin (generic or full)
    54→- Security scanning (cross-language patterns)
    55→- Subjective review (LLM-powered design review)
    56→- Boilerplate + duplicate detection
    57→- Zone classification (test/vendor/config/generated)
    58→- Scoring integration (automatic detector → dimension mapping)
    59→
    60→### Generic plugins with tree-sitter
    61→All of the above, plus:
    62→- Function/method extraction → duplicate detection
    63→- Import parsing → dependency graph → coupling/orphan/cycle detection
    64→- Test coverage analysis (import-based + naming convention mapping)
    65→- AST complexity: nesting depth, cyclomatic complexity, long functions, parameter count, callback depth
    66→- God class/struct detection (methods > 15, LOC > 500, attributes > 10)
    67→- Responsibility cohesion (disconnected function clusters)
    68→- Unused import detection
    69→- Signature variance analysis
    70→- AST smell detection (empty catch, unreachable code)
    71→
    72→### Full plugins additionally get
    73→- Custom per-line smell detectors (language-specific patterns)
    74→- Language-aware auto-fixers
    75→- Custom subjective review dimensions
    76→- Hand-tuned coupling and dependency rules
    77→- Framework-specific detectors (e.g., React hooks, Flutter widgets)
    78→
    79→## The Difference
    80→
    81→A generic Rust plugin runs cargo clippy and gets tree-sitter analysis — that covers most of what you need. A full Python plugin has 15+ hand-written detector phases that understand Python-specific patterns like mutable class variables, `lru_cache` on methods with mutable args, subprocess calls without timeouts, naive regex backtracking, and dozens more. It has auto-fixers that can rewrite imports. It has subjective review dimensions tuned for Python conventions.
    82→
    83→The generic system is the right starting point for any language. Only invest in a full plugin when you need detectors that understand language idioms beyond what AST pattern matching and external linters can catch.
    84→
    85→## Adding a New Generic Plugin
    86→
    87→Create `languages/<name>/__init__.py`:
    88→
    89→```python
    90→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import generic_lang
    91→from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._specs import MY_SPEC  # if available
    92→
    93→generic_lang(
    94→    name="mylang",
    95→    extensions=[".ml"],
    96→    tools=[
    97→        {"label": "mylinter", "cmd": "mylinter --json .",
    98→         "fmt": "json", "id": "mylinter_issue", "tier": 2,
    99→         "fix_cmd": "mylinter --fix ."},
   100→    ],
   101→    exclude=["vendor"],
   102→    detect_markers=["myproject.toml"],
   103→    treesitter_spec=MY_SPEC,
   104→)
   105→```
   106→
   107→That's it. Auto-discovered at startup, zero shared-code edits.
   108→
   109→## Upgrading Generic → Full
   110→
   111→Only needed when you want things generic plugins can't provide: custom per-line smell detectors, language-specific coupling rules, custom fixers with AST rewriting, or full control over phase ordering.
   112→
   113→The path is incremental:
   114→
   115→1. **Start generic** — `generic_lang()` with tool specs + tree-sitter
   116→2. **Extend in-place** — add zone rules, test coverage hooks, security hooks (stays generic)
   117→3. **Go full** — when you need custom detectors or fixers, switch to `@register_lang()` with a package directory
   118→
   119→Bootstrap: `desloppify dev scaffold-lang <name> --extension .ext --marker <root-marker>`
   120→
   121→Required package structure (validated at registration): `__init__.py`, `commands.py`, `extractors.py`, `phases.py`, `move.py`, `review.py`, `test_coverage.py`, plus `detectors/`, `fixers/`, and `tests/` directories.
   122→
   123→A full plugin can still reuse generic building blocks — `_run_tool()` for external tools, tree-sitter extractors, shared phase builders (`detector_phase_security()`, `detector_phase_test_coverage()`, `shared_subjective_duplicates_tail()`).
   124→
   125→## Shared Framework (`_framework/`)
   126→
   127→```
   128→_framework/
   129→├── generic.py           # generic_lang() factory for tool-based plugins
   130→├── base/                # LangConfig, DetectorPhase, FixerConfig contracts
   131→│   ├── types.py         # Core dataclasses
   132→│   ├── shared_phases.py # Shared detector phase runners
   133→│   ├── phase_builders.py # Phase builder helpers
   134→│   └── structural.py   # Structural analysis utilities
   135→├── treesitter/          # Tree-sitter integration (optional)
   136→│   ├── _specs.py        # Per-language tree-sitter query definitions
   137→│   ├── _extractors.py   # Function/class extraction via AST
   138→│   ├── _imports.py      # Import parsing + per-language resolvers
   139→│   ├── _complexity.py   # AST complexity signals
   140→│   ├── _smells.py       # Cross-language AST smell detectors
   141→│   ├── _cohesion.py     # Responsibility cohesion
   142→│   └── _unused_imports.py
   143→├── runtime.py           # LangRun (per-run mutable execution state)
   144→├── resolution.py        # get_lang/available_langs/auto_detect_lang
   145→├── discovery.py         # Plugin auto-discovery
   146→├── commands_base.py     # Shared detect-command factories
   147→└── review_data/         # Shared review dimension JSON payloads
   148→```
   149→
   150→## Design Rules
   151→
   152→- Import direction: `languages/<name>/` → `engine/detectors/` and `languages/_framework/*`. Never the reverse.
   153→- Keep language plugin code in its language folder
   154→- Keep reusable cross-language framework code in `_framework/`
   155→- Generic plugins should NOT mirror the full plugin directory structure — they are intentionally minimal
   156→
   157→## Testing
   158→
   159→```bash
   160→# Run all language tests
   161→pytest -q desloppify/tests/lang/common/ desloppify/languages/*/tests/
   162→
   163→# Run one language's tests
   164→pytest -q desloppify/languages/go/tests/
   165→
   166→# Validate plugin contracts
   167→pytest -q desloppify/tests/lang/common/test_lang_standardization.py
   168→```
   169→
```

1→"""Shared language-framework internals.
     2→
     3→This package contains framework code used by all language plugins:
     4→- config/runtime contracts
     5→- plugin discovery/registration state
     6→- shared detect-command factories
     7→- shared issue factories and facade helpers
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from __future__ import annotations
    11→
    12→from pathlib import Path
    13→
    14→from .base.types import (
    15→    BoundaryRule,
    16→    DetectorPhase,
    17→    FixerConfig,
    18→    FixResult,
    19→    LangConfig,
    20→    LangValueSpec,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→def make_lang_config(name: str, cfg_cls: type) -> LangConfig:
    25→    from .resolution import make_lang_config as _make_lang_config
    26→
    27→    return _make_lang_config(name, cfg_cls)
    28→
    29→
    30→def get_lang(name: str, *, refresh_registry: bool = False) -> LangConfig:
    31→    from .resolution import get_lang as _get_lang
    32→
    33→    if not refresh_registry:
    34→        return _get_lang(name)
    35→    return _get_lang(name, refresh_registry=True)
    36→
    37→
    38→def auto_detect_lang(
    39→    project_root: Path,
    40→    *,
    41→    refresh_registry: bool = False,
    42→) -> str | None:
    43→    from .resolution import auto_detect_lang as _auto_detect_lang
    44→
    45→    if not refresh_registry:
    46→        return _auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    47→    return _auto_detect_lang(project_root, refresh_registry=True)
    48→
    49→
    50→def available_langs(*, refresh_registry: bool = False) -> list[str]:
    51→    from .resolution import available_langs as _available_langs
    52→
    53→    if not refresh_registry:
    54→        return _available_langs()
    55→    return _available_langs(refresh_registry=True)
    56→
    57→__all__ = [
    58→    "BoundaryRule",
    59→    "DetectorPhase",
    60→    "FixerConfig",
    61→    "FixResult",
    62→    "LangConfig",
    63→    "LangValueSpec",
    64→    "auto_detect_lang",

```
# Hermes Agent README

**Hermes Agent** is a self-improving AI agent created by Nous Research. Here's the complete content:

## Overview

The project describes itself as "the only agent with a built-in learning loop — it creates skills from experience, improves them during use, nudges itself to persist knowledge, searches its own past conversations, and builds a deepening model of who you are across sessions."

## Key Features

The platform supports multiple deployment environments—from a $5 VPS to GPU clusters to serverless infrastructure. Users can select from various LLM providers including Nous Portal, OpenRouter (200+ models), OpenAI, and custom endpoints without code modifications.

Notable capabilities include:

- **Interface**: "Full TUI with multiline editing, slash-command autocomplete, conversation history, interrupt-and-redirect, and streaming tool output"
- **Multi-platform access**: Telegram, Discord, Slack, WhatsApp, Signal, and CLI
- **Learning system**: Agent-curated memory, autonomous skill creation, and self-improving capabilities
- **Scheduling**: Built-in cron scheduler for automated tasks
- **Execution**: Multiple terminal backends (local, Docker, SSH, Daytona, Singularity, Modal)

## Installation & Quick Start

Installation uses a bash script handling Python, Node.js, and dependencies. After setup via `hermes setup`, users launch the interactive CLI with `hermes`.

## Documentation & Community

Complete documentation resides at hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs, covering quickstart, CLI usage, configuration, messaging integration, security, tools, skills, memory systems, MCP integration, scheduling, and architecture.

The project welcomes contributions and maintains community channels on Discord and GitHub.

**License**: MIT
```

1→"""Generic language plugin system — run external tools, parse output, emit issues.
     2→
     3→Provides `generic_lang()` to register a language plugin from a list of tool specs.
     4→Each tool runs a shell command at scan time, parses the output into issues, and
     5→gracefully degrades when the tool is not installed or times out.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZoneRule
    13→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_capabilities import (
    15→    SHARED_PHASE_LABELS,
    16→    capability_report,
    17→    generic_zone_rules,
    18→    make_file_finder,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.parsers import (
    21→    PARSERS as _PARSERS,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.parsers import (
    24→    parse_cargo,
    25→    parse_eslint,
    26→    parse_gnu,
    27→    parse_golangci,
    28→    parse_json,
    29→    parse_rubocop,
    30→)
    31→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_factories import (
    32→    make_detect_fn,
    33→    make_tool_phase,
    34→)
    35→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_spec import (
    36→    normalize_tool_specs,
    37→)
    38→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_registration import (
    39→    GenericLangOptions,
    40→    _build_generic_phases,
    41→    _register_generic_tool_specs,
    42→    _resolve_generic_extractors,
    43→)
    44→
    45→
    46→def generic_lang(
    47→    name: str,
    48→    extensions: list[str],
    49→    tools: list[dict[str, Any]],
    50→    *,
    51→    options: GenericLangOptions | None = None,
    52→    exclude: list[str] | None = None,
    53→    depth: str = "shallow",
    54→    detect_markers: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    default_src: str = ".",
    56→    treesitter_spec=None,
    57→    zone_rules: list[ZoneRule] | None = None,
    58→    test_coverage_module: Any | None = None,
    59→) -> LangConfig:
    60→    """Build and register a generic language plugin from tool specs.
    61→
    62→    Each entry in `tools` is::
    63→
    64→        {"label": str, "cmd": str, "fmt": str, "id": str, "tier": int,
    65→         "fix_cmd": str | None}
    66→
    67→    When ``treesitter_spec`` is provided and ``tree-sitter-language-pack`` is
    68→    installed, the plugin gains function extraction (enables duplicate
    69→    detection), and optionally import analysis (enables coupling/orphan/cycle
    70→    detection and test-coverage analysis) for no additional configuration.
    71→
    72→    Returns the built LangConfig (also registered in the language registry).
    73→    """
    74→    opts = options or GenericLangOptions(
    75→        exclude=exclude,
    76→        depth=depth,
    77→        detect_markers=detect_markers,
    78→        default_src=default_src,
    79→        treesitter_spec=treesitter_spec,
    80→        zone_rules=zone_rules,
    81→        test_coverage_module=test_coverage_module,
    82→    )
    83→
    84→    from desloppify.languages import register_generic_lang
    85→
    86→    tool_specs = normalize_tool_specs(tools, supported_formats=set(_PARSERS))
    87→    fixers = _register_generic_tool_specs(tool_specs)
    88→    file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec = _resolve_generic_extractors(
    89→        path_extensions=extensions,
    90→        opts=opts,
    91→    )
    92→    phases = _build_generic_phases(
    93→        tool_specs=tool_specs,
    94→        ts_spec=ts_spec,
    95→        has_treesitter=has_treesitter,
    96→        extract_fn=extract_fn,
    97→        dep_graph_fn=dep_graph_fn,
    98→    )
    99→
   100→    cfg = LangConfig(
   101→        name=name,
   102→        extensions=extensions,
   103→        exclusions=opts.exclude or [],
   104→        default_src=opts.default_src,
   105→        build_dep_graph=dep_graph_fn,
   106→        entry_patterns=[],
   107→        barrel_names=set(),
   108→        phases=phases,
   109→        fixers=fixers,
   110→        get_area=None,
   111→        detect_commands={
   112→            tool["id"]: make_detect_fn(tool["cmd"], _PARSERS[tool["fmt"]])
   113→            for tool in tool_specs
   114→        },
   115→        extract_functions=extract_fn,
   116→        boundaries=[],
   117→        typecheck_cmd="",
   118→        file_finder=file_finder,
   119→        large_threshold=500,
   120→        complexity_threshold=15,
   121→        default_scan_profile="objective",
   122→        detect_markers=opts.detect_markers or [],
   123→        external_test_dirs=["tests", "test"],
   124→        test_file_extensions=extensions,
   125→        zone_rules=opts.zone_rules if opts.zone_rules is not None else generic_zone_rules(extensions),
   126→    )
   127→
   128→    # Set integration depth — upgrade when tree-sitter provides capabilities.
   129→    if has_treesitter and opts.depth in ("shallow", "minimal"):
   130→        cfg.integration_depth = "standard"
   131→    else:
   132→        cfg.integration_depth = opts.depth
   133→
   134→    # Register language-specific test coverage hooks if provided.
   135→    if opts.test_coverage_module is not None:
   136→        from desloppify.engine.hook_registry import register_lang_hooks
   137→
   138→        register_lang_hooks(name, test_coverage=opts.test_coverage_module)
   139→
   140→    register_generic_lang(name, cfg)
   141→    return cfg
   142→
   143→
   144→__all__ = [
   145→    "GenericLangOptions",
   146→    "SHARED_PHASE_LABELS",

1→"""Shared dataclasses and typed records for language framework configs."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import Literal, TypedDict
     9→
    10→
    11→CoverageStatus = Literal["full", "reduced"]
    12→
    13→
    14→class DetectorCoverageRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    """Persisted detector-level coverage confidence metadata."""
    16→
    17→    detector: str
    18→    status: CoverageStatus
    19→    confidence: float
    20→    summary: str
    21→    impact: str
    22→    remediation: str
    23→    tool: str
    24→    reason: str
    25→
    26→
    27→class ScanCoverageRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    28→    """Persisted scan-level coverage snapshot for one language run."""
    29→
    30→    status: CoverageStatus
    31→    confidence: float
    32→    detectors: dict[str, DetectorCoverageRecord]
    33→    warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord]
    34→    updated_at: str
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    38→class DetectorCoverageStatus:
    39→    """Coverage-confidence metadata for a detector in the current scan."""
    40→
    41→    detector: str
    42→    status: CoverageStatus
    43→    confidence: float = 1.0
    44→    summary: str = ""
    45→    impact: str = ""
    46→    remediation: str = ""
    47→    tool: str = ""
    48→    reason: str = ""
    49→
    50→
    51→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    52→class LangSecurityResult:
    53→    """Normalized return shape for language-specific security hooks."""
    54→
    55→    entries: list[dict]
    56→    files_scanned: int
    57→    coverage: DetectorCoverageStatus | None = None
    58→
    59→
    60→@dataclass
    61→class FixResult:
    62→    """Return type for fixer wrappers that need to carry metadata."""
    63→
    64→    entries: list[dict]
    65→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    66→
    67→
    68→@dataclass
    69→class FixerConfig:
    70→    """Configuration for an auto-fixer."""
    71→
    72→    label: str
    73→    detect: Callable[[Path], list[dict]]
    74→    fix: Callable[..., FixResult]
    75→    detector: str  # issue detector name (for state resolution)
    76→    verb: str = "Fixed"
    77→    dry_verb: str = "Would fix"
    78→    # Signature: (path, state, prev_score, dry_run, *, lang=None) -> None
    79→    post_fix: Callable[..., None] | None = None
    80→
    81→
    82→@dataclass
    83→class BoundaryRule:
    84→    """A coupling boundary: `protected` dir should not be imported from `forbidden_from`."""
    85→
    86→    protected: str  # e.g. "shared/"
    87→    forbidden_from: str  # e.g. "tools/"
    88→    label: str  # e.g. "shared→tools"
    89→
    90→
    91→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    92→class LangValueSpec:
    93→    """Typed language option/setting schema entry."""
    94→
    95→    type: type
    96→    default: object
    97→    description: str = ""
    98→
    99→
   100→__all__ = [
   101→    "BoundaryRule",
   102→    "CoverageStatus",
   103→    "DetectorCoverageRecord",
   104→    "DetectorCoverageStatus",
   105→    "FixerConfig",
   106→    "FixResult",
   107→    "LangSecurityResult",

   110→]

1→"""DetectorPhase builder helpers for shared framework phases."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .shared_phases import (
     6→    phase_boilerplate_duplication,
     7→    phase_dupes,
     8→    phase_security,
     9→    phase_signature,
    10→    phase_subjective_review,

    13→from .types import DetectorPhase
    14→
    15→
    16→def _phase_factory(
    17→    label: str,
    18→    run_fn,
    19→    *,
    20→    slow: bool = False,
    21→    exclusive_detector: str | None = None,
    22→):
    23→    """Return a phase factory with explicit label/run/slow semantics.
    24→
    25→    *exclusive_detector*: if set, the detector module path that this phase owns.
    26→    Language-specific phase files must not import that module directly.
    27→    """
    28→    def factory() -> DetectorPhase:
    29→        return DetectorPhase(label, run_fn, slow=slow)
    30→    factory.exclusive_detector = exclusive_detector
    31→    return factory
    32→
    33→
    34→SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES = {
    35→    "test_coverage": _phase_factory(
    36→        "Test coverage", phase_test_coverage,
    37→        exclusive_detector="desloppify.engine.detectors.test_coverage",
    38→    ),
    39→    "security": _phase_factory(
    40→        "Security", phase_security,
    41→        exclusive_detector="desloppify.engine.detectors.security",
    42→    ),
    43→    "signature": _phase_factory(
    44→        "Signature analysis", phase_signature,
    45→        exclusive_detector="desloppify.engine.detectors.signature",
    46→    ),
    47→    "subjective_review": _phase_factory(
    48→        "Subjective review", phase_subjective_review,
    49→        exclusive_detector="desloppify.engine.detectors.review_coverage",
    50→    ),
    51→    "duplicates": _phase_factory(
    52→        "Duplicates", phase_dupes, slow=True,
    53→        exclusive_detector="desloppify.engine.detectors.dupes",
    54→    ),
    55→    "boilerplate_duplication": _phase_factory(
    56→        "Boilerplate duplication", phase_boilerplate_duplication, slow=True,
    57→        exclusive_detector="desloppify.engine.detectors.jscpd_adapter",
    58→    ),
    59→}
    60→
    61→EXCLUSIVE_DETECTOR_MODULES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    62→    f.exclusive_detector
    63→    for f in SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES.values()
    64→    if f.exclusive_detector is not None
    65→)
    66→
    67→
    68→detector_phase_test_coverage = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["test_coverage"]
    69→detector_phase_security = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["security"]
    70→detector_phase_signature = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["signature"]
    71→detector_phase_subjective_review = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["subjective_review"]
    72→detector_phase_duplicates = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["duplicates"]
    73→detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["boilerplate_duplication"]
    74→
    75→
    76→def shared_subjective_duplicates_tail(
    77→    *,
    78→    pre_duplicates: list[DetectorPhase] | None = None,
    79→) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    80→    """Shared review tail: subjective review, optional custom phases, then duplicates."""
    81→    phases = [detector_phase_subjective_review()]
    82→    if pre_duplicates:
    83→        phases.extend(pre_duplicates)
    84→    phases.append(detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication())
    85→    phases.append(detector_phase_duplicates())
    86→    return phases
    87→
    88→
    89→__all__ = [
    90→    "detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication",
    91→    "detector_phase_duplicates",
    92→    "detector_phase_security",
    93→    "detector_phase_signature",
    94→    "detector_phase_subjective_review",
    95→    "detector_phase_test_coverage",
    96→    "EXCLUSIVE_DETECTOR_MODULES",
    97→    "SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES",
    98→    "shared_subjective_duplicates_tail",
    99→]
   100→

```
# Hermes Agent Repository Structure

## Root Level Files and Directories

**Directories:**
- `.github/` - GitHub configuration
- `agent/` - Core agent implementation
- `assets/` - Project assets (banner, images)
- `cron/` - Scheduling functionality
- `datagen-config-examples/` - Data generation examples
- `environments/` - Environment configurations
- `gateway/` - Messaging gateway (Telegram, Discord, etc.)
- `hermes_cli/` - Command-line interface
- `honcho_integration/` - User modeling integration
- `landingpage/` - Web landing page
- `mini-swe-agent/` - Submodule for SWE capabilities
- `optional-skills/` - Optional skill modules
- `scripts/` - Installation and utility scripts
- `skills/` - Core skill definitions
- `tests/` - Test suite
- `tinker-atropos/` - Submodule for RL environments
- `tools/` - Tool implementations (40+ tools)
- `website/` - Documentation website

**Configuration Files:**
- `pyproject.toml`, `requirements.txt` - Python dependencies
- `package.json`, `package-lock.json` - Node.js dependencies
- `uv.lock` - UV package manager lockfile
- `.env.example` - Environment template
- `cli-config.yaml.example` - CLI configuration template
- `.gitignore`, `.gitmodules` - Git configuration

**Core Scripts:**
- `cli.py`, `run_agent.py` - Main entry points
- `setup-hermes.sh` - Installation script
- `batch_runner.py`, `rl_cli.py` - Batch processing utilities
- `hermes_constants.py`, `hermes_state.py`, `hermes_time.py` - State management
- `toolsets.py`, `model_tools.py` - Tool configuration

## Tech Stack Summary

**Primary Language:** Python (84.9%)
**Secondary Languages:** TeX, Shell, PowerShell, HTML, BibTeX

**Key Technologies:**
- Multi-model LLM support (OpenAI, OpenRouter, Nous Portal, etc.)
- Six terminal backends (local, Docker, SSH, Daytona, Singularity, Modal)
- Cross-platform messaging (Telegram, Discord, Slack, WhatsApp, Signal)
- Built-in skill creation and memory systems
- Cron scheduling with platform delivery
- MCP (Model Context Protocol) integration
```

1→"""Python extraction: function bodies, class structure, param patterns."""
     2→
     3→import hashlib
     4→import re
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import find_py_files
     8→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import FunctionInfo
     9→from desloppify.engine.detectors.passthrough import (
    10→    classify_params,
    11→    classify_passthrough_tier,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.languages.python.extractors_classes import extract_py_classes
    14→from desloppify.languages.python.extractors_shared import (
    15→    extract_py_params,
    16→    find_block_end,
    17→    read_file,
    18→)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _find_signature_end(lines: list[str], start: int) -> int | None:
    22→    """Find the line where a function signature closes."""
    23→    for j in range(start, len(lines)):
    24→        lt = lines[j]
    25→        if ")" in lt and ":" in lt[lt.rindex(")") + 1 :]:
    26→            return j
    27→        if j > start and lt.strip().endswith(":"):
    28→            return j
    29→    return None
    30→
    31→
    32→def extract_py_functions(filepath: str) -> list[FunctionInfo]:
    33→    """Extract function bodies from a Python file using indentation-based boundaries."""
    34→    content = read_file(filepath)
    35→    if content is None:
    36→        return []
    37→    lines = content.splitlines()
    38→    functions = []
    39→    fn_re = re.compile(r"^(\s*)(?:async\s+)?def\s+(\w+)\s*\(")
    40→    i = 0
    41→    while i < len(lines):
    42→        m = fn_re.match(lines[i])
    43→        if not m:
    44→            i += 1
    45→            continue
    46→        fn_indent = len(m.group(1))
    47→        name = m.group(2)
    48→        start_line = i
    49→        j = _find_signature_end(lines, i)
    50→        if j is None:
    51→            i += 1
    52→            continue
    53→
    54→        # Extract params from multi-line signature
    55→        sig_text = "\n".join(lines[start_line : j + 1])
    56→        if "(" not in sig_text or ")" not in sig_text:
    57→            i += 1
    58→            continue
    59→        open_paren = sig_text.index("(")
    60→        close_paren = sig_text.rindex(")")
    61→        param_str = sig_text[open_paren + 1 : close_paren]
    62→        params = extract_py_params(param_str)
    63→
    64→        # Find body extent: all lines indented past fn_indent, trim trailing blanks
    65→        block_end = find_block_end(lines, j + 1, fn_indent)
    66→        end_line = block_end
    67→        while end_line > j + 1 and not lines[end_line - 1].strip():
    68→            end_line -= 1
    69→        body = "\n".join(lines[start_line:end_line])
    70→        normalized = normalize_py_body(body)
    71→        if len(normalized.splitlines()) >= 3:
    72→            functions.append(
    73→                FunctionInfo(
    74→                    name=name,
    75→                    file=filepath,
    76→                    line=start_line + 1,
    77→                    end_line=end_line,
    78→                    loc=end_line - start_line,
    79→                    body=body,
    80→                    normalized=normalized,

1→"""Fixer registry assembly for TypeScript."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixerConfig
     8→from desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors import logs as logs_detector_mod
     9→from desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors import smells as smells_detector_mod
    10→from desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors import unused as unused_detector_mod
    11→
    12→_FIXERS_MODULE = "desloppify.languages.typescript.fixers"
    13→
    14→
    15→def _ts_fixers_mod():
    16→    return importlib.import_module(_FIXERS_MODULE)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _det_unused(cat):
    20→    """Create a detector function for a specific unused category."""
    21→
    22→    def f(path):
    23→        return unused_detector_mod.detect_unused(path, category=cat)[0]
    24→
    25→    return f
    26→
    27→
    28→def _det_logs(path):
    29→    """Detect tagged debug logs."""
    30→    return logs_detector_mod.detect_logs(path)[0]
    31→
    32→
    33→def _det_smell(smell_id):
    34→    """Create a detector function for a specific smell ID."""
    35→
    36→    def f(path):
    37→        return next(
    38→            (
    39→                e.get("matches", [])
    40→                for e in smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)[0]
    41→                if e["id"] == smell_id
    42→            ),
    43→            [],
    44→        )
    45→
    46→    return f
    47→
    48→
    49→def _fix_vars(entries, *, dry_run=False):
    50→    """Fix unused vars, returning FixResult."""
    51→    return _ts_fixers_mod().fix_unused_vars(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    52→
    53→
    54→def _fix_logs(entries, *, dry_run=False):
    55→    """Fix debug logs, normalizing result keys."""
    56→    result = _ts_fixers_mod().fix_debug_logs(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    57→    for r in result.entries:
    58→        r["removed"] = r.get("tags", r.get("removed", []))
    59→    return result
    60→
    61→
    62→def get_ts_fixers() -> dict[str, FixerConfig]:
    63→    """Build the TypeScript fixer registry (lazy-loaded)."""
    64→    fixers_mod = _ts_fixers_mod()
    65→    removed, would_remove = "Removed", "Would remove"
    66→    return {
    67→        "unused-imports": FixerConfig(
    68→            "unused imports",
    69→            _det_unused("imports"),
    70→            fixers_mod.fix_unused_imports,
    71→            "unused",
    72→            removed,
    73→            would_remove,
    74→        ),
    75→        "debug-logs": FixerConfig(
    76→            "tagged debug logs",
    77→            _det_logs,
    78→            _fix_logs,
    79→            "logs",
    80→            removed,
    81→            would_remove,
    82→        ),
    83→        "unused-vars": FixerConfig(
    84→            "unused vars",
    85→            _det_unused("vars"),
    86→            _fix_vars,
    87→            "unused",
    88→            removed,
    89→            would_remove,
    90→        ),
    91→        "unused-params": FixerConfig(
    92→            "unused params",
    93→            _det_unused("vars"),
    94→            fixers_mod.fix_unused_params,
    95→            "unused",
    96→            "Prefixed",
    97→            "Would prefix",
    98→        ),
    99→        "dead-useeffect": FixerConfig(
   100→            "dead useEffect calls",
   101→            _det_smell("dead_useeffect"),
   102→            fixers_mod.fix_dead_useeffect,
   103→            "smells",
   104→            removed,
   105→            would_remove,
   106→        ),
   107→        "empty-if-chain": FixerConfig(
   108→            "empty if/else chains",
   109→            _det_smell("empty_if_chain"),
   110→            fixers_mod.fix_empty_if_chain,
   111→            "smells",
   112→            removed,
   113→            would_remove,
   114→        ),
   115→    }
   116→
   117→
   118→__all__ = ["get_ts_fixers"]
   119→

1→"""Detector wiring helpers for TypeScript language configuration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import find_ts_and_tsx_files
     8→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import FunctionInfo
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.phases import make_cohesion_phase
    11→from desloppify.languages.typescript.extractors_functions import extract_ts_functions
    12→
    13→
    14→def ts_treesitter_phases() -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    15→    """Cherry-pick tree-sitter phases that complement TS's own detectors."""
    16→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import get_spec, is_available
    17→
    18→    if not is_available():
    19→        return []
    20→
    21→    spec = get_spec("typescript")
    22→    if spec is None:
    23→        return []
    24→
    25→    return [make_cohesion_phase(spec)]
    26→
    27→
    28→def ts_extract_functions(path: Path) -> list[FunctionInfo]:
    29→    """Extract all TS functions for duplicate detection."""
    30→    functions = []
    31→    for filepath in find_ts_and_tsx_files(path):
    32→        if "node_modules" in filepath or ".d.ts" in filepath:
    33→            continue
    34→        functions.extend(extract_ts_functions(filepath))
    35→    return functions
    36→
    37→
    38→__all__ = ["ts_extract_functions", "ts_treesitter_phases"]
    39→

1→"""Language plugin discovery and import error surfacing."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import importlib.util
     7→import logging
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    11→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    12→
    13→from . import registry_state
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→# Broader than PARSE_INIT_ERRORS: plugin imports may also raise SyntaxError/TypeError.
    18→_PLUGIN_IMPORT_ERRORS: tuple[type[Exception], ...] = (
    19→    ImportError, SyntaxError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError, OSError,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→def _register_module_entrypoint(module: object) -> None:
    24→    """Run a module-level register() entrypoint when provided."""
    25→    register_fn = getattr(module, "register", None)
    26→    if register_fn is None:
    27→        return
    28→    if not callable(register_fn):
    29→        raise TypeError("Language module register entrypoint must be callable")
    30→    register_fn()
    31→
    32→
    33→def raise_load_errors() -> None:
    34→    errors = registry_state.get_load_errors()
    35→    if not errors:
    36→        return
    37→    summaries: list[str] = []
    38→    for module_name, ex in sorted(errors.items()):
    39→        ex_type = type(ex).__name__
    40→        logger.warning(
    41→            "Language plugin %s failed to load: %s: %s",
    42→            module_name,
    43→            ex_type,
    44→            ex,
    45→        )
    46→        summaries.append(f"{module_name}: {ex_type}: {ex}")
    47→    raise ImportError("; ".join(summaries))
    48→
    49→
    50→def _report_load_errors_for_load_all() -> None:
    51→    """Emit warning diagnostics for plugin failures without aborting discovery."""
    52→    try:
    53→        raise_load_errors()
    54→    except ImportError:
    55→        # load_all historically logs plugin failures and continues so command
    56→        # startup can proceed with the languages that did load successfully.
    57→        return
    58→
    59→
    60→def load_all(*, force_reload: bool = False) -> None:
    61→    """Import all language modules to trigger registration."""
    62→    if force_reload:
    63→        registry_state.clear()
    64→    elif registry_state.was_load_attempted():
    65→        _report_load_errors_for_load_all()
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    # Mark load-attempted early to guard against re-entrancy: if a plugin
    69→    # import transitively triggers load_all() again (e.g. via get_lang()),
    70→    # the was_load_attempted() check above will short-circuit instead of
    71→    # re-importing partially-initialised modules.
    72→    registry_state.set_load_attempted(True)
    73→
    74→    lang_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    75→    if lang_dir.name == "_framework":
    76→        lang_dir = lang_dir.parent
    77→    base_package = __package__.rsplit(".", 1)[0]
    78→    failures: dict[str, BaseException] = {}
    79→
    80→    # Discover single-file plugins by naming convention (e.g. plugin_rust.py).
    81→    for f in sorted(lang_dir.glob("plugin_*.py")):
    82→        module_name = f".{f.stem}"
    83→        try:
    84→            module = importlib.import_module(module_name, base_package)
    85→            _register_module_entrypoint(module)
    86→        except _PLUGIN_IMPORT_ERRORS as ex:
    87→            logger.debug("Language plugin import failed for %s: %s", module_name, ex)
    88→            failures[module_name] = ex
    89→
    90→    # Discover packages (e.g. lang/typescript/)
    91→    for d in sorted(lang_dir.iterdir()):
    92→        if (
    93→            d.is_dir()
    94→            and (d / "__init__.py").exists()
    95→            and not d.name.startswith("_")
    96→        ):
    97→            module_name = f".{d.name}"
    98→            try:
    99→                module = importlib.import_module(module_name, base_package)
   100→                _register_module_entrypoint(module)
   101→            except _PLUGIN_IMPORT_ERRORS as ex:
   102→                logger.debug(
   103→                    "Language package import failed for %s: %s", module_name, ex
   104→                )
   105→                failures[module_name] = ex
   106→
   107→    # Discover user plugins from <active-project-root>/.desloppify/plugins/*.py
   108→    try:
   109→        user_plugin_dir = get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "plugins"
   110→        if user_plugin_dir.is_dir():
   111→            for f in sorted(user_plugin_dir.glob("*.py")):
   112→                spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
   113→                    f"desloppify_user_plugin_{f.stem}", f
   114→                )
   115→                if spec and spec.loader:
   116→                    try:
   117→                        mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
   118→                        spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
   119→                        _register_module_entrypoint(mod)
   120→                    except _PLUGIN_IMPORT_ERRORS as ex:
   121→                        logger.debug(
   122→                            "User plugin import failed for %s: %s", f.name, ex
   123→                        )
   124→                        failures[f"user:{f.name}"] = ex
   125→    except (OSError, ImportError) as exc:
   126→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "discover user plugins", exc)
   127→
   128→    registry_state.set_load_errors(failures)
   129→    _report_load_errors_for_load_all()
   130→
   131→
   132→def reload_all() -> None:
   133→    """Force a full in-process language plugin reload."""
   134→    from desloppify.base.registry import reset_registered_detectors
   135→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import reset_registered_scoring_policies
   136→
   137→    reset_registered_detectors()
   138→    reset_registered_scoring_policies()
   139→    load_all(force_reload=True)
   140→
```

1→"""Shared mutable registry state for language plugin discovery."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from contextlib import contextmanager
     6→from contextvars import ContextVar
     7→from collections.abc import ItemsView
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    12→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    13→
    14→__all__ = [
    15→    "RegistryContext",
    16→    "create_registry_context",
    17→    "current_registry_context",
    18→    "registry_context_scope",
    19→    "register",
    20→    "get",
    21→    "all_items",
    22→    "all_keys",
    23→    "is_registered",
    24→    "remove",
    25→    "clear",
    26→    "set_load_attempted",
    27→    "was_load_attempted",
    28→    "record_load_error",
    29→    "set_load_errors",
    30→    "get_load_errors",
    31→]
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class RegistryContext:
    35→    """Mutable language-registry state container."""
    36→
    37→    registry: dict[str, LangConfig] = field(default_factory=dict)
    38→    load_attempted: bool = False
    39→    load_errors: dict[str, BaseException] = field(default_factory=dict)
    40→
    41→
    42→def create_registry_context() -> RegistryContext:
    43→    """Create an isolated language-registry context."""
    44→    return RegistryContext()
    45→
    46→
    47→_STATE = create_registry_context()
    48→_REGISTRY_CONTEXT: ContextVar[RegistryContext] = ContextVar(
    49→    "desloppify_language_registry_context",
    50→    default=_STATE,
    51→)
    52→
    53→
    54→def current_registry_context() -> RegistryContext:
    55→    """Return the active language-registry context."""
    56→    return _REGISTRY_CONTEXT.get()
    57→
    58→
    59→@contextmanager
    60→def registry_context_scope(context: RegistryContext | None = None):
    61→    """Run code with a specific language-registry context."""
    62→    if context is None:
    63→        yield current_registry_context()
    64→        return
    65→    [REDACTED](context)
    66→    try:
    67→        yield context
    68→    finally:
    69→        _REGISTRY_CONTEXT.reset(token)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _ctx(context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> RegistryContext:
    73→    return context if context is not None else current_registry_context()
    74→
    75→# ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────
    76→
    77→
    78→def register(name: str, cfg: LangConfig, *, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> None:
    79→    """Register a language config by name."""
    80→    _ctx(context).registry[name] = cfg
    81→
    82→
    83→def get(name: str, *, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> LangConfig | None:
    84→    """Get a language config by name, or None."""
    85→    return _ctx(context).registry.get(name)
    86→
    87→
    88→def all_items(*, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> ItemsView[str, LangConfig]:
    89→    """Return all (name, config) pairs."""
    90→    return _ctx(context).registry.items()
    91→
    92→
    93→def all_keys(*, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> list[str]:
    94→    """Return all registered language names."""
    95→    return list(_ctx(context).registry.keys())
    96→
    97→
    98→def is_registered(name: str, *, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> bool:
    99→    """Check if a language is registered."""
   100→    return name in _ctx(context).registry
   101→
   102→
   103→def remove(name: str, *, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> None:
   104→    """Remove a language by name (for testing)."""
   105→    _ctx(context).registry.pop(name, None)
   106→
   107→
   108→def clear(*, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> None:
   109→    """Full reset: registrations, load-attempted flag, and load errors."""
   110→    ctx = _ctx(context)
   111→    ctx.registry.clear()
   112→    ctx.load_attempted = False
   113→    ctx.load_errors.clear()
   114→
   115→
   116→def set_load_attempted(value: bool, *, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> None:
   117→    """Set the load-attempted flag."""
   118→    _ctx(context).load_attempted = value
   119→
   120→
   121→def was_load_attempted(*, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> bool:
   122→    """Check whether plugin loading has been attempted."""
   123→    return _ctx(context).load_attempted
   124→
   125→
   126→def record_load_error(
   127→    name: str,
   128→    error: BaseException,
   129→    *,
   130→    context: RegistryContext | None = None,
   131→) -> None:
   132→    """Record an import error for a language module."""
   133→    _ctx(context).load_errors[name] = error
   134→
   135→
   136→def set_load_errors(
   137→    errors: dict[str, BaseException],
   138→    *,
   139→    context: RegistryContext | None = None,
   140→) -> None:
   141→    """Replace the full load-errors dict (used by discovery)."""
   142→    ctx = _ctx(context)
   143→    ctx.load_errors.clear()
   144→    ctx.load_errors.update(errors)
   145→
   146→
   147→def get_load_errors(*, context: RegistryContext | None = None) -> dict[str, BaseException]:
   148→    """Return a copy of the load-errors dict."""
   149→    return dict(_ctx(context).load_errors)
   150→

1→"""Registration and phase-assembly helpers for generic language plugins."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.registry import DetectorMeta, register_detector
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    10→    DetectorScoringPolicy,
    11→    register_scoring_policy,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZoneRule
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, FixerConfig
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_capabilities import (
    16→    empty_dep_graph,
    17→    make_file_finder,
    18→    noop_extract_functions,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_factories import (
    21→    make_generic_fixer,
    22→    make_tool_phase,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_structural import (
    25→    _make_coupling_phase,
    26→    _make_structural_phase,
    27→)
    28→
    29→
    30→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    31→class GenericLangOptions:
    32→    """Optional configuration bundle for generic language registration."""
    33→
    34→    exclude: list[str] | None = None
    35→    depth: str = "shallow"
    36→    detect_markers: list[str] | None = None
    37→    default_src: str = "."
    38→    treesitter_spec: Any | None = None
    39→    zone_rules: list[ZoneRule] | None = None
    40→    test_coverage_module: Any | None = None
    41→
    42→
    43→def _register_generic_tool_specs(tool_specs: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, FixerConfig]:
    44→    fixers: dict[str, FixerConfig] = {}
    45→    for tool in tool_specs:
    46→        has_fixer = tool.get("fix_cmd") is not None
    47→        fixer_name = tool["id"].replace("_", "-") if has_fixer else ""
    48→        register_detector(
    49→            DetectorMeta(
    50→                name=tool["id"],
    51→                display=tool["label"],
    52→                dimension="Code quality",
    53→                action_type="auto_fix" if has_fixer else "manual_fix",
    54→                guidance=f"review and fix {tool['label']} issues",
    55→                fixers=(fixer_name,) if has_fixer else (),
    56→            )
    57→        )
    58→        register_scoring_policy(
    59→            DetectorScoringPolicy(
    60→                detector=tool["id"],
    61→                dimension="Code quality",
    62→                tier=tool["tier"],
    63→                file_based=True,
    64→            )
    65→        )
    66→        if has_fixer:
    67→            fixers[fixer_name] = make_generic_fixer(tool)
    68→    return fixers
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_generic_extractors(
    72→    *,
    73→    path_extensions: list[str],
    74→    opts: GenericLangOptions,
    75→) -> tuple[Any, Any, Any, bool, Any]:
    76→    file_finder = make_file_finder(path_extensions, opts.exclude)
    77→    extract_fn = noop_extract_functions
    78→    dep_graph_fn = empty_dep_graph
    79→    ts_spec = opts.treesitter_spec
    80→    has_treesitter = False
    81→    if ts_spec is None:
    82→        return file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec
    83→
    84→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import is_available
    85→
    86→    if not is_available():
    87→        return file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec
    88→
    89→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._extractors import make_ts_extractor
    90→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._import_graph import make_ts_dep_builder
    91→
    92→    has_treesitter = True
    93→    extract_fn = make_ts_extractor(ts_spec, file_finder)
    94→    if ts_spec.import_query and ts_spec.resolve_import:
    95→        dep_graph_fn = make_ts_dep_builder(ts_spec, file_finder)
    96→    return file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec
    97→
    98→
    99→def _build_generic_phases(
   100→    *,
   101→    tool_specs: list[dict[str, Any]],
   102→    ts_spec: Any,
   103→    has_treesitter: bool,
   104→    extract_fn,
   105→    dep_graph_fn,
   106→) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
   107→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.phase_builders import (
   108→        detector_phase_security,
   109→        detector_phase_test_coverage,
   110→        shared_subjective_duplicates_tail,
   111→    )
   112→
   113→    phases = [
   114→        make_tool_phase(tool["label"], tool["cmd"], tool["fmt"], tool["id"], tool["tier"])
   115→        for tool in tool_specs
   116→    ]
   117→    phases.append(_make_structural_phase(ts_spec if has_treesitter else None))
   118→
   119→    if has_treesitter and ts_spec is not None:
   120→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.phases import (
   121→            make_ast_smells_phase,
   122→            make_cohesion_phase,
   123→            make_unused_imports_phase,
   124→        )
   125→
   126→        phases.append(make_ast_smells_phase(ts_spec))
   127→        phases.append(make_cohesion_phase(ts_spec))
   128→        if ts_spec.import_query:
   129→            phases.append(make_unused_imports_phase(ts_spec))
   130→
   131→    if extract_fn is not noop_extract_functions:
   132→        from desloppify.languages._framework.base.phase_builders import detector_phase_signature
   133→
   134→        phases.append(detector_phase_signature())
   135→
   136→    phases.append(detector_phase_security())
   137→    if dep_graph_fn is not empty_dep_graph:
   138→        phases.append(_make_coupling_phase(dep_graph_fn))
   139→        phases.append(detector_phase_test_coverage())
   140→
   141→    phases.extend(shared_subjective_duplicates_tail())
   142→    return phases
   143→

1→"""Capability and stub helpers for generic language plugins."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import find_source_files
    10→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import FunctionInfo
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import COMMON_ZONE_RULES, Zone, ZoneRule
    12→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    13→
    14→# Shared phase labels — used by capability_report and langs command.
    15→SHARED_PHASE_LABELS = frozenset(
    16→    {
    17→        "Security",
    18→        "Subjective review",
    19→        "Boilerplate duplication",
    20→        "Duplicates",
    21→        "Structural analysis",
    22→        "Coupling + cycles + orphaned",
    23→        "Test coverage",
    24→        "AST smells",
    25→        "Responsibility cohesion",
    26→        "Unused imports",
    27→        "Signature analysis",
    28→    }
    29→)
    30→
    31→
    32→def make_file_finder(
    33→    extensions: list[str], exclusions: list[str] | None = None
    34→) -> Callable:
    35→    """Return a file finder function for the given extensions."""
    36→    excl = exclusions or []
    37→
    38→    def finder(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
    39→        return find_source_files(path, extensions, excl or None)
    40→
    41→    return finder
    42→
    43→
    44→def empty_dep_graph(path: Path) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    45→    """Stub dep graph builder — generic plugins have no import parsing."""
    46→    del path
    47→    return {}
    48→
    49→
    50→def noop_extract_functions(path: Path) -> list[FunctionInfo]:
    51→    """Stub function extractor — generic plugins don't extract functions."""
    52→    del path
    53→    return []
    54→
    55→
    56→def generic_zone_rules(extensions: list[str]) -> list[ZoneRule]:
    57→    """Minimal zone rules: test dirs -> test, vendor/node_modules -> vendor, plus common."""
    58→    del extensions
    59→    return [
    60→        ZoneRule(Zone.VENDOR, ["/node_modules/"]),
    61→    ] + COMMON_ZONE_RULES
    62→
    63→
    64→def capability_report(cfg: LangConfig) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]] | None:
    65→    """Return (present, missing) capability lists. None for full plugins."""
    66→    if cfg.integration_depth == "full":
    67→        return None
    68→
    69→    phase_labels = {p.label for p in cfg.phases}
    70→    present: list[str] = []
    71→    missing: list[str] = []
    72→
    73→    def check(condition: bool, label: str) -> None:
    74→        (present if condition else missing).append(label)
    75→
    76→    tool_phases = [p.label for p in cfg.phases if p.label not in SHARED_PHASE_LABELS]
    77→    check(
    78→        bool(tool_phases),
    79→        f"linting ({', '.join(tool_phases)})" if tool_phases else "linting",
    80→    )
    81→    check(bool(cfg.fixers), "auto-fix")
    82→    check(cfg.build_dep_graph is not empty_dep_graph, "import analysis")
    83→    check(cfg.extract_functions is not noop_extract_functions, "function extraction")
    84→    check("Security" in phase_labels, "security scan")
    85→    check("Boilerplate duplication" in phase_labels, "boilerplate detection")
    86→    check("Subjective review" in phase_labels, "design review")
    87→
    88→    return present, missing
    89→
    90→
    91→__all__ = [
    92→    "SHARED_PHASE_LABELS",
    93→    "capability_report",
    94→    "empty_dep_graph",
    95→    "generic_zone_rules",
    96→    "make_file_finder",
    97→    "noop_extract_functions",
    98→]
    99→

1→"""Config instantiation and public language resolution helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from contextlib import contextmanager
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→import desloppify.base.registry as detector_registry_mod
    10→import desloppify.engine.hook_registry as hook_registry_mod
    11→
    12→from . import registry_state
    13→from .base.types import LangConfig
    14→from .contract_validation import validate_lang_contract
    15→from .discovery import load_all
    16→
    17→_MARKER_GLOB_CHARS = ("*", "?", "[")
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class RegistrySession:
    22→    """Scoped mutable registries for discovery/resolution flows."""
    23→
    24→    language_registry: registry_state.RegistryContext = field(
    25→        default_factory=registry_state.create_registry_context
    26→    )
    27→    hook_registry: hook_registry_mod.HookRegistryContext = field(
    28→        default_factory=hook_registry_mod.create_hook_registry_context
    29→    )
    30→    detector_registry: detector_registry_mod.DetectorRegistryContext = field(
    31→        default_factory=detector_registry_mod.create_detector_registry_context
    32→    )
    33→
    34→
    35→def create_registry_session() -> RegistrySession:
    36→    """Create a fresh scoped registry session."""
    37→    return RegistrySession()
    38→
    39→
    40→@contextmanager
    41→def _session_scope(session: RegistrySession | None = None):
    42→    if session is None:
    43→        yield
    44→        return
    45→    with registry_state.registry_context_scope(session.language_registry):
    46→        with hook_registry_mod.hook_registry_scope(session.hook_registry):
    47→            with detector_registry_mod.detector_registry_scope(
    48→                session.detector_registry
    49→            ):
    50→                yield
    51→
    52→
    53→def _reset_dynamic_registries_for_refresh(
    54→    *,
    55→    session: RegistrySession | None = None,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    """Reset mutable registries before refresh-driven discovery."""
    58→    from desloppify.base.registry import reset_registered_detectors
    59→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import reset_registered_scoring_policies
    60→
    61→    reset_registered_detectors(
    62→        context=(None if session is None else session.detector_registry)
    63→    )
    64→    reset_registered_scoring_policies()
    65→    hook_registry_mod.clear_lang_hooks_for_tests(
    66→        context=(None if session is None else session.hook_registry)
    67→    )
    68→
    69→
    70→def make_lang_config(name: str, cfg_cls: type) -> LangConfig:
    71→    """Instantiate and validate a language config."""
    72→    try:
    73→        cfg = cfg_cls()
    74→    except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, OSError) as ex:
    75→        raise ValueError(
    76→            f"Failed to instantiate language config '{name}': {ex}"
    77→        ) from ex
    78→    validate_lang_contract(name, cfg)
    79→    return cfg
    80→
    81→
    82→def get_lang(
    83→    name: str,
    84→    *,
    85→    refresh_registry: bool = False,
    86→    session: RegistrySession | None = None,
    87→) -> LangConfig:
    88→    """Get a language config by name.
    89→
    90→    All plugins (full and generic) store LangConfig instances in the registry.
    91→    Test doubles that store plain classes are instantiated on demand as a fallback.
    92→    """
    93→    with _session_scope(session):
    94→        if refresh_registry:
    95→            _reset_dynamic_registries_for_refresh(session=session)
    96→            load_all(force_reload=True)
    97→        elif not registry_state.is_registered(name):
    98→            load_all()
    99→        if not registry_state.is_registered(name):
   100→            available = ", ".join(sorted(registry_state.all_keys()))
   101→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown language: {name!r}. Available: {available}")
   102→        obj = registry_state.get(name)
   103→        if isinstance(obj, LangConfig):
   104→            return obj
   105→        return make_lang_config(name, obj)  # fallback for test doubles
   106→
   107→
   108→def auto_detect_lang(
   109→    project_root: Path,
   110→    *,
   111→    refresh_registry: bool = False,
   112→    session: RegistrySession | None = None,
   113→) -> str | None:
   114→    """Auto-detect language from project files.
   115→
   116→    When multiple config files are present (e.g. package.json + pyproject.toml),
   117→    counts actual source files to pick the dominant language instead of relying
   118→    on first-match ordering.
   119→    """
   120→    with _session_scope(session):
   121→        if refresh_registry:
   122→            _reset_dynamic_registries_for_refresh(session=session)
   123→        load_all(force_reload=refresh_registry)
   124→        candidates: list[str] = []
   125→        configs: dict[str, LangConfig] = {}
   126→
   127→        for lang_name, obj in registry_state.all_items():
   128→            cfg = obj if isinstance(obj, LangConfig) else make_lang_config(lang_name, obj)
   129→            configs[lang_name] = cfg
   130→            markers = getattr(cfg, "detect_markers", []) or []
   131→            if markers and any(_detect_marker_exists(project_root, marker) for marker in markers):
   132→                candidates.append(lang_name)
   133→
   134→        if not candidates:
   135→            # Marker-less fallback: pick language with most source files.
   136→            best, best_count = None, 0
   137→            for lang_name, cfg in configs.items():
   138→                count = len(cfg.file_finder(project_root))
   139→                if count > best_count:
   140→                    best, best_count = lang_name, count
   141→            return best if best_count > 0 else None
   142→
   143→        if len(candidates) == 1:
   144→            return candidates[0]
   145→
   146→        # Multiple candidates: choose language with most source files.
   147→        best, best_count = None, -1
   148→        for lang_name in candidates:
   149→            count = len(configs[lang_name].file_finder(project_root))
   150→            if count > best_count:
   151→                best, best_count = lang_name, count
   152→        return best
   153→
   154→
   155→def _detect_marker_exists(project_root: Path, marker: str) -> bool:
   156→    marker_text = str(marker).strip()
   157→    if not marker_text:
   158→        return False
   159→
   160→    # Fast path for literal markers.
   161→    if (project_root / marker_text).exists():
   162→        return True
   163→
   164→    # Wildcard markers (for example "*.fsproj") are matched at project root.
   165→    if any(ch in marker_text for ch in _MARKER_GLOB_CHARS):
   166→        return any(project_root.glob(marker_text))
   167→    return False
   168→
   169→
   170→def available_langs(
   171→    *,
   172→    refresh_registry: bool = False,
   173→    session: RegistrySession | None = None,
   174→) -> list[str]:
   175→    """Return list of registered language names."""
   176→    with _session_scope(session):
   177→        if refresh_registry:
   178→            _reset_dynamic_registries_for_refresh(session=session)
   179→        load_all(force_reload=refresh_registry)
   180→        return sorted(registry_state.all_keys())
   181→
```

# Hermes Agent pyproject.toml Overview

This is a Python project configuration file for **hermes-agent**, described as "The self-improving AI agent — creates skills from experience, improves them during use, and runs anywhere."

## Key Project Details

**Metadata:**
- Version: 0.1.0
- Python requirement: ≥3.11
- Author: Nous Research
- License: MIT

## Core Dependencies

The project requires fundamental packages including OpenAI integration, environment configuration (python-dotenv), CLI tools (fire, prompt_toolkit), web utilities (httpx, requests), and data handling (pyyaml, pydantic). Additional components support text-to-speech via edge-tts, web scraping through firecrawl-py, and AI model routing via litellm.

## Optional Features

The configuration provides modular installation options:
- **Modal/Daytona**: Cloud execution environments
- **Messaging**: Telegram, Discord, Slack, and Aiohttp integrations
- **Premium TTS**: Elevenlabs support
- **Development**: Testing frameworks (pytest, pytest-asyncio)
- **MCP**: Model context protocol support
- **Home Assistant**: Smart home integration

## CLI Entry Points

Two command-line interfaces are defined:
- `hermes`: Routes to hermes_cli.main:main
- `hermes-agent`: Routes to run_agent:main

## Testing Configuration

Tests are located in the `tests` directory, with integration tests marked separately and excluded by default from standard test runs.

# Complete File Contents: run_agent.py

This is a comprehensive Python module implementing `AIAgent`, a sophisticated AI agent framework with tool-calling capabilities. The file is approximately 3,000+ lines and contains the complete agent implementation.

## Key Components

**Core Class: AIAgent**
The main agent class managing conversation flow, tool execution, and response handling with support for multiple model providers (OpenRouter, OpenAI, local models).

**Notable Features:**
- "Thread-safe iteration budget tracking across parent and child agents"
- Context compression with automatic activation at 85% of model context limits
- Prompt caching for Claude models via OpenRouter (reducing costs ~75%)
- Interrupt mechanism for graceful early termination
- Session persistence to JSON and SQLite databases
- Support for both chat_completions and codex_responses API modes
- Fallback provider system with automatic failover
- Integration with Honcho AI for persistent user modeling
- Memory management (MEMORY.md and USER.md files)
- Todo list management for task planning

**Provider Support:**
- OpenRouter (primary)
- Direct OpenAI
- Nous (OAuth-based)
- OpenAI Codex (OAuth-based)
- Kimi API
- Custom endpoints

**Advanced Features:**
- Anthropic prompt caching with cache breakpoints
- Native reasoning token extraction from multiple provider formats
- Codex Responses API with streaming and reasoning items
- Context compression with configurable thresholds
- Trajectory saving for model training/analysis
- Redacting formatter for sensitive data in logs

The implementation emphasizes reliability, observability, and graceful degradation with extensive error handling and fallback mechanisms throughout.
```

1→"""Tree-sitter integration — optional, gracefully degrades when not installed.
     2→
     3→Install with: pip install tree-sitter-language-pack
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import logging
     9→from collections.abc import Callable
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→_AVAILABLE = False
    17→try:
    18→    import tree_sitter_language_pack  # noqa: F401
    19→
    20→    _AVAILABLE = True
    21→except ImportError as exc:
    22→    log_best_effort_failure(logger, "import tree_sitter_language_pack", exc)
    23→
    24→
    25→def is_available() -> bool:
    26→    """Return True if tree-sitter-language-pack is installed."""
    27→    return _AVAILABLE
    28→
    29→
    30→def enable_parse_cache() -> None:
    31→    """Enable scan-scoped parse tree cache."""
    32→    from ._cache import enable_parse_cache as _enable
    33→
    34→    _enable()
    35→
    36→
    37→def disable_parse_cache() -> None:
    38→    """Disable parse tree cache and free memory."""
    39→    from ._cache import disable_parse_cache as _disable
    40→
    41→    _disable()
    42→
    43→
    44→def is_parse_cache_enabled() -> bool:
    45→    """Check if parse cache is currently enabled."""
    46→    from ._cache import is_parse_cache_enabled as _is_enabled
    47→
    48→    return _is_enabled()
    49→
    50→
    51→def _build_treesitter_specs() -> dict[str, TreeSitterLangSpec]:
    52→    """Build the TREESITTER_SPECS dict from the three spec modules."""
    53→    from . import _specs_compiled as _sc, _specs_functional as _sf, _specs_scripting as _ss
    54→
    55→    return {
    56→        "go": _sc.GO_SPEC, "rust": _sc.RUST_SPEC, "java": _sc.JAVA_SPEC,
    57→        "kotlin": _sc.KOTLIN_SPEC, "csharp": _sc.CSHARP_SPEC,
    58→        "swift": _sc.SWIFT_SPEC, "php": _sc.PHP_SPEC, "dart": _sc.DART_SPEC,
    59→        "c": _sc.C_SPEC, "cpp": _sc.CPP_SPEC, "scala": _sc.SCALA_SPEC,
    60→        "elixir": _sf.ELIXIR_SPEC, "erlang": _sf.ERLANG_SPEC,
    61→        "fsharp": _sf.FSHARP_SPEC, "haskell": _sf.HASKELL_SPEC,
    62→        "ocaml": _sf.OCAML_SPEC, "clojure": _sf.CLOJURE_SPEC,
    63→        "ruby": _ss.RUBY_SPEC, "javascript": _ss.JS_SPEC,
    64→        "typescript": _ss.TYPESCRIPT_SPEC, "bash": _ss.BASH_SPEC,
    65→        "lua": _ss.LUA_SPEC, "perl": _ss.PERL_SPEC, "zig": _ss.ZIG_SPEC,
    66→        "nim": _ss.NIM_SPEC, "powershell": _ss.POWERSHELL_SPEC,
    67→        "gdscript": _ss.GDSCRIPT_SPEC, "r": _ss.R_SPEC,
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def get_spec(language: str) -> TreeSitterLangSpec | None:
    72→    """Return tree-sitter spec for a language key, if configured."""
    73→    key = str(language or "").strip().lower()
    74→    if not key:
    75→        return None
    76→    return _build_treesitter_specs().get(key)
    77→
    78→
    79→def list_specs() -> dict[str, TreeSitterLangSpec]:
    80→    """Return a shallow copy of the public tree-sitter spec registry."""
    81→    return dict(_build_treesitter_specs())
    82→
    83→
    84→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    85→class TreeSitterLangSpec:
    86→    """Per-language tree-sitter configuration.
    87→
    88→    Fields:
    89→        grammar: tree-sitter grammar name (e.g. "go", "rust")
    90→        function_query: S-expression query capturing @func, @name, @body
    91→        comment_node_types: AST node types considered comments
    92→        string_node_types: AST node types considered strings (for normalization)
    93→        import_query: S-expression query capturing @import and @path
    94→        resolve_import: (import_text, source_file, scan_path) -> abs_path | None
    95→        class_query: S-expression query capturing @class, @name, @body
    96→        log_patterns: regexes for log/debug lines to strip during normalization
    97→    """
    98→
    99→    grammar: str
   100→    function_query: str
   101→    comment_node_types: frozenset[str]
   102→    string_node_types: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
   103→
   104→    import_query: str = ""
   105→    resolve_import: Callable[[str, str, str], str | None] | None = None
   106→
   107→    class_query: str = ""
   108→
   109→    log_patterns: tuple[str, ...] = (
   110→        r"^\s*(?:fmt\.Print|log\.)",
   111→        r"^\s*(?:println!|eprintln!|dbg!)",
   112→        r"^\s*(?:puts |p |pp )",
   113→        r"^\s*(?:print\(|NSLog)",
   114→        r"^\s*(?:System\.out\.|Logger\.)",
   115→        r"^\s*console\.",
   116→    )
   117→
   118→
   119→# Common exception tuple for tree-sitter parser/query initialisation failures.
   120→# Used across all treesitter modules to avoid repeating the same 4-tuple.
   121→PARSE_INIT_ERRORS: tuple[type[Exception], ...] = (
   122→    ImportError, OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError
   123→)
   124→
   125→__all__ = [
   126→    "PARSE_INIT_ERRORS",
   127→    "TreeSitterLangSpec",
   128→    "disable_parse_cache",
   129→    "enable_parse_cache",
   130→    "get_spec",
   131→    "is_available",
   132→    "is_parse_cache_enabled",
   133→    "list_specs",
   134→]
   135→
   136→# Re-export phase factories for convenience.
   137→# Actual definitions live in .phases to avoid circular imports at import time.
   138→def __getattr__(name: str):  # noqa: N807
   139→    _PHASE_EXPORTS = {
   140→        "all_treesitter_phases",
   141→        "make_ast_smells_phase",
   142→        "make_cohesion_phase",
   143→        "make_unused_imports_phase",
   144→    }
   145→    if name in _PHASE_EXPORTS:
   146→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import phases as phases_mod
   147→
   148→        return getattr(phases_mod, name)
   149→    if name == "TREESITTER_SPECS":
   150→        return _build_treesitter_specs()
   151→    if name.endswith("_SPEC"):
   152→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   153→            _specs_compiled as _sc,
   154→            _specs_functional as _sf,
   155→            _specs_scripting as _ss,
   156→        )
   157→
   158→        for _mod in (_sc, _sf, _ss):
   159→            if hasattr(_mod, name):
   160→                return getattr(_mod, name)
   161→    raise AttributeError(f"module {__name__!r} has no attribute {name!r}")
   162→
