```
1→"""Python import graph builder — parses import/from statements, resolves to files."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import ast
     6→import logging
     7→from collections import defaultdict
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import resolve_path
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import find_py_files
    14→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    15→from desloppify.engine.detectors.graph import finalize_graph
    16→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors.deps_dynamic import (
    17→    find_python_dynamic_imports,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors.deps_resolution import (
    20→    resolve_python_from_import as _resolve_python_from_import,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors.deps_resolution import (
    23→    resolve_python_import as _resolve_python_import,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→def build_dep_graph(
    29→    path: Path,
    30→    roslyn_cmd: str | None = None,
    31→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    32→    """Build a dependency graph for Python files.
    33→
    34→    Uses ast.parse for reliable import extraction (handles multi-line imports,
    35→    parenthesized imports, aliases, etc.).
    36→
    37→    Returns {resolved_path: {"imports": set, "importers": set, "import_count", "importer_count"}}
    38→    """
    39→    del roslyn_cmd
    40→    py_files = find_py_files(path)
    41→
    42→    graph: dict[str, dict] = defaultdict(
    43→        lambda: {
    44→            "imports": set(),
    45→            "importers": set(),
    46→            "deferred_imports": set(),
    47→        }
    48→    )
    49→
    50→    for filepath in py_files:
    51→        abs_path = (
    52→            filepath if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else str(get_project_root() / filepath)
    53→        )
    54→        try:
    55→            content = Path(abs_path).read_text()
    56→            tree = ast.parse(content, filename=abs_path)
    57→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, SyntaxError) as exc:
    58→            logger.debug(
    59→                "Skipping unreadable/unparseable python file %s in deps detector: %s",
    60→                filepath,
    61→                exc,
    62→            )
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        source_resolved = resolve_path(filepath)
    66→        graph[source_resolved]  # ensure entry
    67→
    68→        # Collect top-level function/class line ranges to detect deferred imports
    69→        top_level_scopes: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
    70→        for node in ast.iter_child_nodes(tree):
    71→            if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef | ast.ClassDef):
    72→                end = getattr(node, "end_lineno", node.lineno)
    73→                top_level_scopes.append((node.lineno, end))
    74→
    75→        for node in ast.walk(tree):
    76→            if not isinstance(node, ast.Import | ast.ImportFrom):
    77→                continue
    78→
    79→            is_deferred = any(
    80→                start <= node.lineno <= end for start, end in top_level_scopes
    81→            )
    82→
    83→            if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
    84→                # Build module_path from level (dots) + module name
    85→                dots = "." * (node.level or 0)
    86→                module = node.module or ""
    87→                module_path = dots + module
    88→
    89→                import_names = ", ".join(a.name for a in node.names)
    90→                targets = _resolve_python_from_import(
    91→                    module_path, import_names, filepath, path
    92→                )
    93→
    94→                for target in targets:
    95→                    graph[source_resolved]["imports"].add(target)
    96→                    graph[target]["importers"].add(source_resolved)
    97→                    if is_deferred:
    98→                        graph[source_resolved]["deferred_imports"].add(target)
    99→
   100→            elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
   101→                for alias in node.names:
   102→                    target = _resolve_python_import(alias.name, filepath, path)
   103→                    if target:
   104→                        graph[source_resolved]["imports"].add(target)
   105→                        graph[target]["importers"].add(source_resolved)
   106→                        if is_deferred:
   107→                            graph[source_resolved]["deferred_imports"].add(target)
   108→
   109→    return finalize_graph(dict(graph))
   110→
   111→__all__ = ["build_dep_graph", "find_python_dynamic_imports"]
   112→
```

```
    13→
    14→def _validate_core_identity(name: str, cfg: LangConfig) -> list[str]:
    15→    errors: list[str] = []
    16→    if cfg.name != name:
    17→        errors.append(f"config name mismatch: expected '{name}', got '{cfg.name}'")
    18→    if not cfg.extensions:
    19→        errors.append("extensions must be non-empty")
    20→    if not callable(cfg.build_dep_graph):
    21→        errors.append("build_dep_graph must be callable")
    22→    if not callable(cfg.file_finder):
    23→        errors.append("file_finder must be callable")
    24→    if not callable(cfg.extract_functions):
    25→        errors.append("extract_functions must be callable")
    26→    if cfg.default_scan_profile not in ALLOWED_SCAN_PROFILES:
    27→        allowed = ", ".join(sorted(ALLOWED_SCAN_PROFILES))
    28→        errors.append(f"default_scan_profile must be one of: {allowed}")
    29→    return errors
    30→
    31→
    32→def _validate_phases(cfg: LangConfig) -> list[str]:
    33→    errors: list[str] = []
    34→    if not cfg.phases:
    35→        errors.append("phases must be non-empty")
    36→        return errors
    37→
    38→    for idx, phase in enumerate(cfg.phases):
    39→        if not isinstance(phase, DetectorPhase):
    40→            errors.append(f"phase[{idx}] is not DetectorPhase")
    41→            continue
    42→        if not isinstance(phase.label, str) or not phase.label.strip():
    43→            errors.append(f"phase[{idx}] has empty label")
    44→        if not callable(phase.run):
    45→            errors.append(f"phase[{idx}] run must be callable")
    46→    return errors
    47→
    48→
    49→def _validate_detect_commands(cfg: LangConfig) -> list[str]:
    50→    errors: list[str] = []
    51→    if not isinstance(cfg.detect_commands, dict) or not cfg.detect_commands:
    52→        errors.append("detect_commands must be a non-empty dict")
    53→        return errors
    54→
    55→    for key, fn in cfg.detect_commands.items():
    56→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
    57→            errors.append("detect_commands has empty/non-string key")
    58→        elif (
    59→            key != key.lower()
    60→            or "-" in key
    61→            or not all(ch.isalnum() or ch == "_" for ch in key)
    62→        ):
    63→            errors.append(f"detect command '{key}' must use lowercase snake_case")
    64→        if not callable(fn):
    65→            errors.append(f"detect command '{key}' is not callable")
    66→    return errors
    67→
    68→

    71→    if not isinstance(cfg.fixers, dict):
    72→        errors.append("fixers must be a dict")
    73→        return errors
    74→
    75→    for key, fixer in cfg.fixers.items():
    76→        if not isinstance(fixer, FixerConfig):
    77→            errors.append(f"fixer '{key}' is not FixerConfig")
    78→    return errors
    79→
    80→
    81→def _validate_setting_specs(cfg: LangConfig) -> list[str]:
    82→    errors: list[str] = []
    83→    if not isinstance(cfg.setting_specs, dict):
    84→        errors.append("setting_specs must be a dict")
    85→        return errors
    86→
    87→    for key, spec in cfg.setting_specs.items():
    88→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
    89→            errors.append("setting_specs has empty/non-string key")
    90→        if not isinstance(spec, LangValueSpec):
    91→            errors.append(f"setting_specs['{key}'] is not LangValueSpec")
    92→    return errors
    93→
    94→
    95→def _validate_runtime_option_specs(cfg: LangConfig) -> list[str]:
    96→    errors: list[str] = []
    97→    if not isinstance(cfg.runtime_option_specs, dict):
    98→        errors.append("runtime_option_specs must be a dict")
    99→        return errors
   100→
   101→    for key, spec in cfg.runtime_option_specs.items():
   102→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   103→            errors.append("runtime_option_specs has empty/non-string key")
   104→        if not isinstance(spec, LangValueSpec):
   105→            errors.append(f"runtime_option_specs['{key}'] is not LangValueSpec")
   106→    return errors
   107→
   108→
   109→def _validate_zone_rules(cfg: LangConfig) -> list[str]:
   110→    return [] if cfg.zone_rules else ["zone_rules must be non-empty"]
   111→
   112→
   113→def validate_lang_contract(name: str, cfg: LangConfig) -> None:
   114→    """Validate LangConfig runtime contract so broken plugins fail fast."""
   115→    errors: list[str] = []
   116→
   117→    if not isinstance(cfg, LangConfig):
   118→        errors.append(f"plugin class for '{name}' must return LangConfig")
   119→    else:
   120→        errors.extend(_validate_core_identity(name, cfg))

   124→        errors.extend(_validate_setting_specs(cfg))
   125→        errors.extend(_validate_runtime_option_specs(cfg))
   126→        errors.extend(_validate_zone_rules(cfg))
   127→
   128→    if errors:
   129→        raise ValueError(
   130→            f"Language plugin '{name}' has invalid LangConfig contract:\n"
   131→            + "\n".join(f"  - {e}" for e in errors)
   132→        )
```

```
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.languages._framework.policy import REQUIRED_DIRS, REQUIRED_FILES
     8→
     9→
    10→def validate_lang_structure(lang_dir: Path, name: str) -> None:
    11→    """Validate that a language plugin has all required files and directories."""
    12→    errors: list[str] = []
    13→
    14→    for filename in REQUIRED_FILES:
    15→        if not (lang_dir / filename).is_file():
    16→            errors.append(f"missing required file: {filename}")
    17→
    18→    for dirname in REQUIRED_DIRS:
    19→        target = lang_dir / dirname
    20→        if not target.is_dir():
    21→            errors.append(f"missing required directory: {dirname}/")
    22→            continue
    23→        if not (target / "__init__.py").is_file():
    24→            errors.append(f"missing {dirname}/__init__.py")
    25→        if dirname == "tests" and not any(target.glob("test_*.py")):
    26→            errors.append("tests directory must contain at least one test_*.py file")
    27→
    28→    if errors:
    29→        raise ValueError(
    30→            f"Language plugin '{name}' ({lang_dir.name}/) has structural issues:\n"
    31→            + "\n".join(f"  - {e}" for e in errors)
    32→        )
    33→
```

```
"""Zone classification system — deterministic file intent classification.

Classifies files into zones (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)
based on path patterns. Zone metadata flows through issues, scoring, and the LLM.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum

from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
from desloppify.engine.policy.zones_data import (
    CONFIG_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    SCRIPT_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
    TEST_SKIP_DETECTORS,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Zone(str, Enum):
    """File intent zone — determines scoring and detection policy."""

    PRODUCTION = "production"
    TEST = "test"
    CONFIG = "config"
    GENERATED = "generated"
    SCRIPT = "script"
    VENDOR = "vendor"


# Zones excluded from the health score
EXCLUDED_ZONES = {Zone.TEST, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR}

# String values for quick lookup in scoring (issues store zone as string)
EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES = {z.value for z in EXCLUDED_ZONES}


@dataclass
class ZoneRule:
    """A classification rule: zone + list of path patterns.

    Patterns are matched against relative file paths. First matching rule wins.
    Pattern types (auto-detected from shape):
      - "/dir/"   → substring match on full path (directory marker)
      - ".ext"    → basename ends-with (suffix/extension, e.g. ".d.ts", ".test.")
      - "prefix_" → basename starts-with (trailing underscore)
      - "name.py" → basename exact match (has extension, no /)
      - fallback  → substring on full path
    """

    zone: Zone
    patterns: list[str]


def _match_pattern(rel_path: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
    """Match a zone pattern against a relative file path.

    See ZoneRule docstring for pattern type conventions.
    """
    basename = os.path.basename(rel_path)

    # Directory pattern: "/dir/" → substring on padded path
    if pattern.startswith("/") and pattern.endswith("/"):
        return pattern in ("/" + rel_path + "/")

    # Suffix/extension pattern: starts with "." → contains on basename
    if pattern.startswith("."):
        return pattern in basename

    # Prefix pattern: ends with "_" → basename starts-with
    if pattern.endswith("_"):
        return basename.startswith(pattern)

    # Suffix pattern: starts with "_" → basename ends-with (_test.py, _pb2.py)
    if pattern.startswith("_"):
        return basename.endswith(pattern)

    # Exact basename: has a proper file extension (1-5 chars after last dot),
    # no "/" → exact basename match (config.py, setup.py, conftest.py)
    if "/" not in pattern and "." in pattern:
        ext = pattern.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
        if ext and len(ext) <= 5 and ext.isalnum():
            return basename == pattern

    # Fallback: substring on full path (vite.config, tsconfig, eslint, etc.)
    return pattern in rel_path


# ── Common zone rules (shared across languages) ──────────

COMMON_ZONE_RULES = [
    ZoneRule(Zone.VENDOR, ["/vendor/", "/third_party/", "/vendored/"]),
    ZoneRule(Zone.GENERATED, ["/generated/", "/__generated__/"]),
    ZoneRule(Zone.TEST, ["/tests/", "/test/", "/fixtures/"]),
```

```
    17→
    18→def _output_file_has_text(output_file: Path) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when the output file exists and contains non-empty text."""
    20→    if not output_file.exists():
    21→        return False
    22→    try:
    23→        return len(output_file.read_text().strip()) > 0
    24→    except OSError:
    25→        return False
    26→
    27→
    28→def run_triage_stage(
    29→    *,
    30→    prompt: str,
    31→    repo_root: Path,
    32→    output_file: Path,
    33→    log_file: Path,
    34→    timeout_seconds: int = 1800,
    35→    validate_output_fn: Callable[[Path], bool] | None = None,
    36→) -> int:
    37→    """Execute a triage stage via codex subprocess. Returns exit code."""
    38→    normalized_prompt = str(prompt).strip()
    39→    if not normalized_prompt:
    40→        safe_write_text(log_file, "Empty triage prompt — skipping execution.\n")
    41→        return 2
    42→    output_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    43→    log_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    44→    if validate_output_fn is None:
    45→        validate_output_fn = _output_file_has_text
    46→    timeout = timeout_seconds if timeout_seconds > 0 else 1800
    47→    preview = " ".join(
    48→        codex_batch_command(
    49→            prompt=normalized_prompt,
    50→            repo_root=repo_root,
    51→            output_file=output_file,
    52→        )
    53→    )
    54→    safe_write_text(log_file, f"RUNNER COMMAND PREVIEW:\n{preview}\n")
    55→    deps = CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
    56→        timeout_seconds=timeout,
    57→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
    58→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    59→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
    60→        use_popen_runner=True,
    61→        subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
    62→        live_log_interval_seconds=10.0,
    63→        stall_after_output_seconds=120,
    64→        max_retries=1,
    65→        retry_backoff_seconds=5.0,
    66→        sleep_fn=time.sleep,
    67→        validate_output_fn=validate_output_fn,
    68→    )
    69→    return run_codex_batch(
    70→        prompt=normalized_prompt,
    71→        repo_root=repo_root,
    72→        output_file=output_file,
    73→        log_file=log_file,
    74→        deps=deps,
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→__all__ = ["run_triage_stage"]
    79→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for triage runner orchestrators."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→
    10→from ..helpers import has_triage_in_queue, inject_triage_stages
    11→from ..services import TriageServices
    12→
    13→STAGES: tuple[str, ...] = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
    14→
    15→
    16→def parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    17→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    18→    if not raw:
    19→        return list(STAGES)
    20→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    21→    for stage in stages:
    22→        if stage not in STAGES:
    23→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {', '.join(STAGES)}")
    24→    return stages
    25→
    26→
    27→def run_stamp() -> str:
    28→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    29→
    30→
    31→def ensure_triage_started(
    32→    plan: dict[str, Any],
    33→    services: TriageServices,
    34→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    35→    """Auto-start triage if not started. Returns updated plan."""
    36→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    37→        inject_triage_stages(plan)
    38→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    39→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    40→        services.save_plan(plan)
    41→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started.", "cyan"))
    42→    return plan
    43→
```

```
1→"""Shared parallel-batch execution helper for codex triage stages."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_parallel import (
     8→    BatchExecutionOptions,
     9→    BatchProgressEvent,
    10→    execute_batches,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→
    14→
    15→def run_parallel_batches(
    16→    *,
    17→    tasks: dict[int, object],
    18→    stage_label: str,
    19→    batch_label_fn: Callable[[int], str],
    20→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None],
    21→    heartbeat_seconds: float = 15.0,
    22→    heartbeat_printer: Callable[[BatchProgressEvent], None] | None = None,
    23→) -> list[int]:
    24→    """Execute parallel codex batches with shared progress/error logging."""
    25→
    26→    stage_slug = stage_label.lower().replace(" ", "-")
    27→
    28→    def _progress(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
    29→        idx = event.batch_index
    30→        label = batch_label_fn(idx)
    31→        if event.event == "start":
    32→            print(colorize(f"    {stage_label} {label} started", "dim"))
    33→            append_run_log(f"{stage_slug}-batch-start {label}")
    34→        elif event.event == "done":
    35→            elapsed = event.details.get("elapsed_seconds", 0) if event.details else 0
    36→            status = "done" if event.code == 0 else f"failed ({event.code})"
    37→            tone = "dim" if event.code == 0 else "yellow"
    38→            print(colorize(f"    {stage_label} {label} {status} in {int(elapsed)}s", tone))
    39→            append_run_log(
    40→                f"{stage_slug}-batch-done {label} code={event.code} elapsed={int(elapsed)}s"
    41→            )
    42→        elif event.event == "heartbeat" and heartbeat_printer is not None:
    43→            heartbeat_printer(event)
    44→
    45→    def _error_log(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
    46→        append_run_log(f"{stage_slug}-batch-error batch={batch_index} error={exc}")
    47→
    48→    return execute_batches(
    49→        tasks=tasks,
    50→        options=BatchExecutionOptions(
    51→            run_parallel=True,
    52→            heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
    53→        ),
    54→        progress_fn=_progress,
    55→        error_log_fn=_error_log,
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→__all__ = ["run_parallel_batches"]
    60→
```

```
    21→
    22→def _merge_observe_outputs(
    23→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
    24→) -> str:
    25→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
    26→    parts: list[str] = []
    27→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
    28→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
    29→        content = ""
    30→        if output_file.exists():
    31→            try:
    32→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
    33→            except OSError:
    34→                content = "(batch output missing)"
    35→        if not content:
    36→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
    37→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
    38→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
    39→
    40→
    41→def run_observe(
    42→    *,
    43→    si,
    44→    repo_root: Path,
    45→    prompts_dir: Path,
    46→    output_dir: Path,
    47→    logs_dir: Path,

    54→
    55→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
    56→    total = len(batches)
    57→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
    58→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
    59→
    60→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
    61→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
    62→
    63→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
    64→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
    65→            batch_index=i + 1,
    66→            total_batches=total,
    67→            dimension_group=dims,
    68→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
    69→            repo_root=repo_root,
    70→        )
    71→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
    72→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
    73→
    74→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
    75→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
    76→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
    77→
    78→        if not dry_run:
    79→            tasks[i] = partial(
    80→                run_triage_stage,
    81→                prompt=prompt,
    82→                repo_root=repo_root,
    83→                output_file=output_file,
    84→                log_file=log_file,
    85→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
    86→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
    87→            )
    88→
    89→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
    90→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
    91→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
    92→
    93→    if dry_run:
    94→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
    95→        return True, ""
    96→
    97→    def _heartbeat(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
    98→        details = event.details or {}
    99→        active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   100→        elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   101→        if active:
   102→            parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   104→
   105→    def _batch_label(idx: int) -> str:
   106→        return f"batch {idx + 1}/{total}"
   107→
   108→    failures = run_parallel_batches(
   109→        tasks=tasks,
   110→        stage_label="Observe",
   111→        batch_label_fn=_batch_label,
   112→        append_run_log=_log,
   113→        heartbeat_seconds=15.0,
   114→        heartbeat_printer=_heartbeat,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    if failures:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}", "red"))
   119→        for idx in failures:
   120→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   121→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   122→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   123→        return False, ""
   124→
   125→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   126→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   127→    _log(f"observe-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
```

```
    26→def _is_full_stage_run(stages_to_run: list[str]) -> bool:
    27→    """True when the pipeline was asked to run the full triage stage set."""
    28→    return set(stages_to_run) == set(STAGES)
    29→
    30→
    31→def _all_stage_results_successful(
    32→    *,
    33→    stages_to_run: list[str],
    34→    stage_results: dict[str, dict],
    35→) -> bool:
    36→    """True when each requested stage is confirmed or already confirmed."""
    37→    for stage in stages_to_run:
    38→        status = str(stage_results.get(stage, {}).get("status", ""))
    39→        if status not in {"confirmed", "skipped"}:
    40→            return False
    41→    return True
    42→
    43→
    44→def _print_not_finalized_message(reason: str) -> None:
    45→    """Emit a consistent next-step message when auto-completion is skipped/blocked."""
    46→    print(colorize(f"\n  Stages complete, triage not finalized ({reason}).", "yellow"))
    47→    print(
    48→        colorize(
    49→            '  Finalize manually: desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "<execution plan>"',
    50→            "dim",
    51→        )
    52→    )
    53→
    54→
    55→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    56→class StageHandler:
    57→    """Per-stage execution/record hooks for the codex triage pipeline."""
    58→
    59→    run_parallel: Callable[..., tuple[bool | None, str | None]] | None = None
    60→    record_report: Callable[[str, argparse.Namespace, TriageServices], None] | None = None
    61→
    62→
    63→def _run_observe_parallel(
    64→    *,
    65→    si: dict,
    66→    plan: dict,
    67→    repo_root: Path,
    68→    prompts_dir: Path,
    69→    output_dir: Path,
    70→    logs_dir: Path,
    71→    timeout_seconds: int,
    72→    dry_run: bool,
    73→    append_run_log,
    74→) -> tuple[bool | None, str | None]:
    75→    del plan
    76→    return run_observe(
    77→        si=si,
    78→        repo_root=repo_root,
    79→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
    80→        output_dir=output_dir,
    81→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
    82→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
    83→        dry_run=dry_run,
    84→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
    85→    )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _record_observe_report(
    89→    report: str,
    90→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    91→    services: TriageServices,
    92→) -> None:

    94→
    95→    record_args = argparse.Namespace(
    96→        stage="observe",
    97→        report=report,
    98→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
    99→    )
   100→    cmd_stage_observe(record_args, services=services)

   102→
   103→def _run_sense_check_parallel(
   104→    *,
   105→    si: dict,
   106→    plan: dict,
   107→    repo_root: Path,
   108→    prompts_dir: Path,
   109→    output_dir: Path,
   110→    logs_dir: Path,
   111→    timeout_seconds: int,
   112→    dry_run: bool,
   113→    append_run_log,
   114→) -> tuple[bool | None, str | None]:
   115→    del si

   121→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   122→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   123→        dry_run=dry_run,
   124→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   125→    )

   128→def _record_sense_check_report(
   129→    report: str,
   130→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   131→    services: TriageServices,
   132→) -> None:
   133→    from ..stage_flow_commands import cmd_stage_sense_check
   134→
   135→    record_args = argparse.Namespace(
   136→        stage="sense-check",
   137→        report=report,
   138→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   139→    )
   140→    cmd_stage_sense_check(record_args, services=services)
   141→
   142→
   143→_STAGE_HANDLERS: dict[str, StageHandler] = {
   144→    "observe": StageHandler(
   145→        run_parallel=_run_observe_parallel,
   146→        record_report=_record_observe_report,
   147→    ),
   148→    "sense-check": StageHandler(
   149→        run_parallel=_run_sense_check_parallel,
   150→        record_report=_record_sense_check_report,
   151→    ),
   152→}
   153→
   154→
   155→def _execute_stage(
   156→    *,
   157→    stage: str,
   158→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   159→    services: TriageServices,
   160→    plan: dict,
   161→    si: dict,
   162→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   163→    repo_root: Path,
   164→    prompts_dir: Path,
   165→    output_dir: Path,
   166→    logs_dir: Path,
   167→    stage_start: float,
   168→    timeout_seconds: int,
   169→    dry_run: bool,
   170→    append_run_log,
   171→) -> tuple[str, dict]:
   172→    """Execute one stage and return (status, stage_result)."""
   173→    from .codex_runner import run_triage_stage
   174→
   175→    handler = _STAGE_HANDLERS.get(stage)
   176→    used_parallel = False
   177→
   178→    if handler and handler.run_parallel is not None:
   179→        parallel_ok, merged_report = handler.run_parallel(
   180→            si=si,
   181→            plan=plan,
   182→            repo_root=repo_root,
   183→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   184→            output_dir=output_dir,
   185→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   186→            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   187→            dry_run=dry_run,
   188→            append_run_log=append_run_log,
   189→        )
   190→        if parallel_ok is True and dry_run:
   191→            return "dry_run", {"status": "dry_run"}
   192→        if parallel_ok is True and merged_report:
   193→            if handler.record_report is not None:
   194→                handler.record_report(merged_report, args, services)
   195→            used_parallel = True
   196→        elif parallel_ok is False:
   197→            elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   198→            print(
   199→                colorize(f"  {stage.capitalize()}: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red")
   200→            )
   201→            append_run_log(
   202→                f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s reason=parallel_execution_failed"
   203→            )
   204→            return "failed", {"status": "failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   205→
   206→    if not used_parallel:
   207→        prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root)
   208→
   209→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   210→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   211→
   212→        if dry_run:
   213→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: prompt written to {prompt_file}", "cyan"))
   214→            print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute codex subprocess.", "dim"))
   215→            return "dry_run", {"status": "dry_run"}
   216→
   217→        print(colorize(f"\n  Stage {stage}: launching codex subprocess...", "bold"))
   218→        append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-start stage={stage}")
   219→
   220→        output_file = output_dir / f"{stage}.raw.txt"
   221→        log_file = logs_dir / f"{stage}.log"
   222→
   223→        exit_code = run_triage_stage(
   224→            prompt=prompt,
   225→            repo_root=repo_root,
   226→            output_file=output_file,
   227→            log_file=log_file,
   228→            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   232→        append_run_log(
   233→            f"stage-subprocess-done stage={stage} code={exit_code} elapsed={elapsed}s"
   234→        )
   235→
   236→        if exit_code != 0:
   237→            print(
   238→                colorize(
   239→                    f"  Stage {stage}: codex subprocess failed (exit {exit_code}).", "red"
   240→                )
   241→            )
   242→            print(colorize(f"  Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   243→            print(colorize("  Re-run to resume (confirmed stages are skipped).", "dim"))
   244→            append_run_log(
   245→                f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s code={exit_code}"
   246→            )
   247→            return "failed", {
   248→                "status": "failed",
   249→                "exit_code": exit_code,
   250→                "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   251→            }
   252→
   253→    return "ready", {}
   254→
   255→
   256→def _validate_and_confirm_stage(
   257→    *,
   258→    stage: str,
   259→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   260→    services: TriageServices,
   261→    si: dict,
   262→    state,
   263→    repo_root: Path,
   264→    stage_start: float,
   265→    append_run_log,
   266→) -> tuple[bool, dict, str]:
   267→    """Run shared stage validation + confirmation flow."""
   268→    plan = services.load_plan()
   269→
   270→    ok, error_msg = validate_stage(stage, plan, state, repo_root, triage_input=si)
   271→    if not ok:
   272→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   273→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: validation failed: {error_msg}", "red"))
   274→        print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   275→        append_run_log(
   276→            f"stage-validation-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s error={error_msg}"
   277→        )
   278→        return (
   279→            False,
   280→            {
   281→                "status": "validation_failed",
   282→                "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   283→                "error": error_msg,
   284→            },
   285→            "",
   286→        )
   287→
   288→    attestation = build_auto_attestation(stage, plan, si)
   289→    confirm_args = argparse.Namespace(
   290→        confirm=stage,
   291→        attestation=attestation,
   292→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   293→    )
   294→
   295→    from ..confirmations_router import cmd_confirm_stage
   296→
   297→    cmd_confirm_stage(confirm_args, services=services)
   298→
   299→    plan = services.load_plan()
   300→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   301→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   302→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   303→    if stage in stages_data and stages_data[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   304→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: confirmed ({elapsed}s).", "green"))
   305→        append_run_log(f"stage-confirmed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   306→        report = stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", "")
   307→        return (
   308→            True,
   309→            {"status": "confirmed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed},
   310→            report,
   311→        )
   312→
   313→    print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: auto-confirmation did not take effect.", "red"))
   314→    print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   315→    append_run_log(f"stage-confirm-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   316→    return (
   317→        False,
   318→        {"status": "confirm_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed},
   319→        "",
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def _build_completion_strategy(stages_data: dict[str, dict]) -> str:
   324→    strategy_parts: list[str] = []
   325→    for stage in STAGES:
   326→        report = stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", "")
   327→        if report:
   328→            strategy_parts.append(f"[{stage}] {report[:200]}")
   329→    strategy = " ".join(strategy_parts)
   330→    if len(strategy) < 200:
   331→        strategy = strategy + " " + "Automated triage via codex subagent pipeline. " * 3
   332→    return strategy
   333→
   334→
   335→def _complete_pipeline(
   336→    *,
   337→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   338→    services: TriageServices,
   339→    plan: dict,
   340→    strategy: str,
   341→    triage_input: dict,
   342→) -> bool:
   343→    """Run the triage completion coordinator and report success."""
   344→    completed_before = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("last_completed_at")
   345→
   346→    print(colorize("\n  Completing triage...", "bold"))
   347→
   348→    attestation = build_auto_attestation("sense-check", plan, triage_input)
   349→    complete_args = argparse.Namespace(
   350→        complete=True,
   351→        strategy=strategy[:2000],
   352→        attestation=attestation,
   353→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   354→    )
   355→
   356→    from ..stage_completion_commands import _cmd_triage_complete
   357→
   358→    _cmd_triage_complete(complete_args, services=services)
   359→
   360→    completed_after = (
   361→        services.load_plan().get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("last_completed_at")
   362→    )
   363→    return bool(completed_after and completed_after != completed_before)
   364→
   365→
   366→def run_codex_pipeline(
   367→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   368→    *,
   369→    stages_to_run: list[str],
   370→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   371→) -> None:
   372→    """Run triage stages via Codex subprocesses (automated pipeline)."""
   373→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   374→    timeout_seconds = int(getattr(args, "stage_timeout_seconds", 1800) or 1800)
   375→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   376→
   377→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   378→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   379→    ensure_triage_started(plan, resolved_services)
   380→
   381→    stamp = run_stamp()
   382→    desloppify_dir = repo_root / ".desloppify"
   383→    run_dir = desloppify_dir / "triage_runs" / stamp
   384→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   385→    output_dir = run_dir / "output"
   386→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   387→    for d in (prompts_dir, output_dir, logs_dir):
   388→        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   389→
   390→    run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   391→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   392→    append_run_log(
   393→        f"run-start runner=codex stages={','.join(stages_to_run)} "
   394→        f"timeout={timeout_seconds}s dry_run={dry_run}"
   395→    )
   396→
   397→    print(colorize(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   398→    print(colorize(f"  Live run log:  {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   399→
   400→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   401→    state = runtime.state
   402→
   403→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   404→    stage_results: dict[str, dict] = {}
   405→    pipeline_start = time.monotonic()
   406→
   407→    for stage in stages_to_run:
   408→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   409→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   410→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   411→
   412→        if stage in stages and stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   413→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: already confirmed, skipping.", "green"))
   414→            append_run_log(f"stage-skip stage={stage} reason=already_confirmed")
   415→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "skipped"}
   416→            report = stages[stage].get("report", "")
   417→            if report:
   418→                prior_reports[stage] = report
   419→            continue
   420→
   421→        stage_start = time.monotonic()
   422→        append_run_log(f"stage-start stage={stage}")
   423→
   424→        si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   425→        exec_status, exec_result = _execute_stage(
   426→            stage=stage,
   427→            args=args,
   428→            services=resolved_services,
   429→            plan=plan,
   430→            si=si,
   431→            prior_reports=prior_reports,
   432→            repo_root=repo_root,
   433→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   434→            output_dir=output_dir,
   435→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   436→            stage_start=stage_start,
   437→            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   438→            dry_run=dry_run,
   439→            append_run_log=append_run_log,
   440→        )
   441→        if exec_status == "dry_run":
   442→            stage_results[stage] = exec_result
   443→            continue
   444→        if exec_status == "failed":
   445→            stage_results[stage] = exec_result
   446→            write_triage_run_summary(
   447→                run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log
   448→            )
   449→            return
   450→
   451→        confirmed, confirm_result, report = _validate_and_confirm_stage(
   452→            stage=stage,
   453→            args=args,
   454→            services=resolved_services,
   455→            si=si,
   456→            state=state,
   457→            repo_root=repo_root,
   458→            stage_start=stage_start,
   459→            append_run_log=append_run_log,
   460→        )
   461→        stage_results[stage] = confirm_result
   462→        if not confirmed:
   463→            write_triage_run_summary(
   464→                run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log
   465→            )
   466→            return
   467→        if report:
   468→            prior_reports[stage] = report
   469→
   470→    if dry_run:
   471→        print(colorize("\n  [dry-run] All prompts generated. No stages executed.", "cyan"))
   472→        write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   473→        return
   474→
   475→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   476→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   477→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   478→
   479→    strategy = _build_completion_strategy(stages_data)
   480→
   481→    should_auto_complete = (
   482→        _is_full_stage_run(stages_to_run)
   483→        and _all_stage_results_successful(
   484→            stages_to_run=stages_to_run,
   485→            stage_results=stage_results,
   486→        )
   487→    )
   488→    if not should_auto_complete:
   489→        total_elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - pipeline_start)
   490→        _print_not_finalized_message("partial stage run")
   491→        append_run_log(f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s finalized=false reason=partial_stage_run")
   492→        write_triage_run_summary(
   493→            run_dir,
   494→            stamp,
   495→            stages_to_run,
   496→            stage_results,
   497→            append_run_log,
   498→            finalized=False,
   499→            finalization_reason="partial_stage_run",
   500→        )
   501→        return
   502→
   503→    completed = _complete_pipeline(
   504→        args=args,
   505→        services=resolved_services,
   506→        plan=plan,
   507→        strategy=strategy,
   508→        triage_input=si,
   509→    )
   510→    total_elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - pipeline_start)
   511→    if not completed:
   512→        _print_not_finalized_message("completion command blocked")
   513→        append_run_log(
   514→            f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s finalized=false reason=completion_blocked"
   515→        )
   516→        write_triage_run_summary(
   517→            run_dir,
   518→            stamp,
   519→            stages_to_run,
   520→            stage_results,
   521→            append_run_log,
   522→            finalized=False,
   523→            finalization_reason="completion_blocked",
   524→        )
   525→        return
   526→
   527→    print(colorize(f"\n  Triage pipeline complete ({total_elapsed}s).", "green"))
   528→    append_run_log(f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s finalized=true")
   529→    write_triage_run_summary(
   530→        run_dir,
   531→        stamp,
   532→        stages_to_run,
   533→        stage_results,
   534→        append_run_log,
   535→        finalized=True,
   536→    )
   537→
   538→
   539→def write_triage_run_summary(
   540→    run_dir: Path,
   541→    stamp: str,
   542→    stages: list[str],
   543→    stage_results: dict[str, dict],
   544→    append_run_log,
   545→    *,
   546→    finalized: bool | None = None,
   547→    finalization_reason: str | None = None,
   548→) -> None:
   549→    """Write a run_summary.json with per-stage results."""
   550→    summary = {
   551→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   552→        "run_stamp": stamp,
   553→        "runner": "codex",
   554→        "stages_requested": stages,
   555→        "stage_results": stage_results,
   556→        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   557→    }
   558→    if finalized is not None:
   559→        summary["finalized"] = finalized
   560→    if finalization_reason:
   561→        summary["finalization_reason"] = finalization_reason
   562→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   563→    safe_write_text(summary_path, json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   564→    print(colorize(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   565→    append_run_log(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   566→
   567→
   568→__all__ = ["run_codex_pipeline"]
   569→
```

```
1→"""Sense-check prompt builders for triage runner."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→
     8→def build_sense_check_content_prompt(
     9→    *,
    10→    cluster_name: str,
    11→    plan: dict,
    12→    repo_root: Path,
    13→    policy_block: str = "",
    14→) -> str:
    15→    """Build a content-verification prompt for a single cluster."""
    16→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
    17→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    18→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    19→
    20→    parts: list[str] = []
    21→    parts.append(
    22→        f"You are sense-checking cluster `{cluster_name}` "
    23→        f"({len(steps)} steps, {len(issue_ids)} issues).\n"
    24→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
    25→    )
    26→
    27→    parts.append(
    28→        "## Your job\n"
    29→        "For EVERY step in this cluster, read the actual source file and verify\n"
    30→        "every factual claim. Then fix anything wrong or vague.\n"
    31→    )
    32→
    33→    parts.append(
    34→        "## What to check and fix\n"
    35→        "1. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match what the step describes?\n"
    36→        "   Fix: update the line range to match current file state.\n"
    37→        "2. NAMES: Do the function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?\n"
    38→        "   Fix: correct the names.\n"
    39→        "3. COUNTS: \"Update the 3 imports\" — are there actually 3? Or 5?\n"
    40→        "   Fix: correct the count.\n"
    41→        "4. STALENESS: Is the problem the issue describes still present in the code?\n"
    42→        "   If already fixed, note in your report.\n"
    43→        "5. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step without\n"
    44→        "   asking a single question? If not:\n"
    45→        "   - Replace \"refactor X\" with the specific transformation\n"
    46→        "   - Replace \"update imports\" with the specific file list\n"
    47→        "   - Replace \"extract into new hook\" with the filename, function signature, return type\n"
    48→        "6. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match the actual scope? A one-line rename is \"trivial\",\n"
    49→        "   not \"small\". Decomposing a 400-line file is \"large\", not \"medium\".\n"
    50→        "7. DUPLICATES: If you notice this step does the same thing as a step in another\n"
    51→        "   cluster, note it in your report.\n"
    52→        "8. OVER-ENGINEERING: Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:\n"
    53→        "   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation\n"
    54→        "   - Introduce unnecessary config, feature flags, or generalization\n"
    55→        "   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit\n"
    56→        "   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires\n"
    57→        "   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)\n"
    58→        "   If a step is net-negative, recommend removing it or simplifying the approach.\n"
    59→        "   If the entire cluster is net-negative, say so clearly in your report.\n"
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    if policy_block:
    63→        parts.append(policy_block)
    64→
    65→    parts.append(
    66→        "## How to fix\n"
    67→        f"desloppify plan cluster update {cluster_name} "
    68→        "--update-step N --detail \"corrected...\" --effort <tag>\n"
    69→    )
    70→
    71→    parts.append(
    72→        "## What NOT to do\n"
    73→        "- Do NOT reorder steps (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
    74→        "- Do NOT add --depends-on (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
    75→        "- Do NOT add new steps for missing cascade updates (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
    76→        "- Do NOT run any other desloppify commands\n"
    77→    )
    78→
    79→    # Include cluster steps
    80→    parts.append("## Current Steps\n")
    81→    for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    82→        title = step.get("title", str(step)) if isinstance(step, dict) else str(step)
    83→        detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
    84→        effort = step.get("effort", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
    85→        refs = step.get("issue_refs", []) if isinstance(step, dict) else []
    86→        line = f"{i}. **{title}**"
    87→        if effort:
    88→            line += f" [{effort}]"
    89→        if refs:
    90→            line += f" (refs: {', '.join(refs[:3])})"
    91→        if detail:
    92→            line += f"\n   {detail[:300]}"
    93→        parts.append(line)
    94→
    95→    parts.append(
    96→        "\n## Output\n"
    97→        "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
    98→    )
    99→
   100→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   101→
   102→
   103→def build_sense_check_structure_prompt(
   104→    *,
   105→    plan: dict,
   106→    repo_root: Path,
   107→) -> str:
   108→    """Build a structure-verification prompt for cross-cluster dependency checking."""
   109→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   110→
   111→    parts: list[str] = []
   112→    parts.append(
   113→        "You are checking cross-cluster dependencies for the entire triage plan.\n"
   114→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    parts.append(
   118→        "## Your job\n"
   119→        "Build a file-touch graph: for each cluster, which files do its steps reference?\n"
   120→        "Then check for unsafe relationships between clusters.\n"
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    parts.append(
   124→        "## What to check and fix\n"
   125→        "1. SHARED FILES: If cluster A and cluster B both have steps touching the same file,\n"
   126→        "   and neither depends on the other → add a dependency.\n"
   127→        "   Fix: desloppify plan cluster update {later_cluster} --depends-on {earlier_cluster}\n"
   128→        "2. MISSING CASCADE: If a step renames/removes a function or export, check whether\n"
   129→        "   any other file imports it. If those importers aren't covered by any step in any\n"
   130→        "   cluster → add a cascade step.\n"
   131→        "   Fix: desloppify plan cluster update {cluster} --add-step \"Update importers of {name}\"\n"
   132→        "        --detail \"Files importing {old}: {list}. Update import to {new}.\"\n"
   133→        "        --effort trivial --issue-refs {hash}\n"
   134→        "3. CIRCULAR DEPS: If adding a dependency would create a cycle, flag it in your report\n"
   135→        "   instead of adding it.\n"
   136→    )
   137→
   138→    parts.append(
   139→        "## What NOT to do\n"
   140→        "- Do NOT modify step detail text (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   141→        "- Do NOT change effort tags (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   142→        "- Do NOT remove steps or deduplicate (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   143→        "- Do NOT run any other desloppify commands besides cluster update --depends-on and --add-step\n"
   144→    )
   145→
   146→    # Include all clusters with their steps and dependencies
   147→    parts.append("## Clusters\n")
   148→    for name, c in sorted(clusters.items()):
   149→        if c.get("auto"):
   150→            continue
   151→        steps = c.get("action_steps", [])
   152→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   153→        issues = c.get("issue_ids", [])
   154→        header = f"### {name} ({len(steps)} steps, {len(issues)} issues)"
   155→        if deps:
   156→            header += f"\n  depends_on: {', '.join(deps)}"
   157→        parts.append(header)
   158→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   159→            title = step.get("title", str(step)) if isinstance(step, dict) else str(step)
   160→            detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   161→            line = f"  {i}. {title}"
   162→            if detail:
   163→                line += f"\n     {detail[:200]}"
   164→            parts.append(line)
   165→
   166→    parts.append(
   167→        "\n## Output\n"
   168→        "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   172→
   173→
   174→__all__ = [
   175→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   176→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   177→]
   178→
```

```
1→"""Post-subagent validation and auto-attestation for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import TriageInput
     9→
    10→from ..helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues, observe_dimension_breakdown
    11→from ..stage_helpers import unclustered_review_issues, unenriched_clusters
    12→from .._stage_validation import (
    13→    _cluster_file_overlaps,
    14→    _clusters_with_directory_scatter,
    15→    _clusters_with_high_step_ratio,
    16→    _underspecified_steps,
    17→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    18→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    19→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    20→    _steps_without_effort,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class EnrichQualityFailure:
    26→    """Structured enrich-quality validation failure."""
    27→
    28→    code: str
    29→    message: str
    30→
    31→
    32→def run_enrich_quality_checks(
    33→    plan: dict,
    34→    repo_root: Path,
    35→    *,
    36→    phase_label: str,
    37→) -> list[EnrichQualityFailure]:
    38→    """Run enrich-level executor-readiness checks for a phase."""
    39→    sense_suffix = f" after {phase_label}" if phase_label == "sense-check" else ""
    40→    failures: list[EnrichQualityFailure] = []
    41→
    42→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    43→    if underspec:
    44→        total = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
    45→        failures.append(
    46→            EnrichQualityFailure(
    47→                code="underspecified_steps",
    48→                message=f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs{sense_suffix}.",
    49→            )
    50→        )
    51→
    52→    bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
    53→    if bad_paths:
    54→        total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
    55→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    56→            message = f"{total} file path(s) don't exist on disk{sense_suffix}."
    57→        else:
    58→            message = f"{total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk."
    59→        failures.append(
    60→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="missing_paths", message=message)
    61→        )
    62→
    63→    untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
    64→    if untagged:
    65→        total = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
    66→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    67→            message = f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag{sense_suffix}."
    68→        else:
    69→            message = (
    70→                f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag "
    71→                "(trivial/small/medium/large)."
    72→            )
    73→        failures.append(
    74→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="missing_effort", message=message)
    75→        )
    76→
    77→    no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
    78→    if no_refs:
    79→        total = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
    80→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    81→            message = f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs{sense_suffix}."
    82→        else:
    83→            message = f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs for traceability."
    84→        failures.append(
    85→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="missing_issue_refs", message=message)
    86→        )
    87→
    88→    vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
    89→    if vague:
    90→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    91→            message = f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail{sense_suffix}."
    92→        else:
    93→            message = (
    94→                f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths). "
    95→                "Executor-ready means: file path + specific instruction."
    96→            )
    97→        failures.append(
    98→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="vague_detail", message=message)
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    return failures
   102→
   103→
   104→def validate_stage(
   105→    stage: str,
   106→    plan: dict,
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    repo_root: Path,
   109→    *,
   110→    triage_input: TriageInput | None = None,
   111→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   112→    """Check subagent completed stage correctly. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   113→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   114→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   115→
   116→    if stage == "observe":
   117→        if "observe" not in stages:
   118→            return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
   119→        report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   120→        if len(report) < 100:
   121→            return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   122→        # Check citation ratio — must reference specific issues
   123→        cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
   124→        issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
   125→        if issue_count > 0:
   126→            min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
   127→            if len(cited) < min_citations:
   128→                return False, (
   129→                    f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
   130→                    f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
   131→                    f"hashes to prove you read them."
   132→                )
   133→        return True, ""
   134→
   135→    if stage == "reflect":
   136→        if "reflect" not in stages:
   137→            return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
   138→        report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   139→        if len(report) < 100:
   140→            return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   141→        return True, ""
   142→
   143→    if stage == "organize":
   144→        if "organize" not in stages:
   145→            return False, "Organize stage not recorded."
   146→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   147→        if not manual:
   148→            return False, "No manual clusters with issues exist."
   149→        gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   150→        if gaps:
   151→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _ in gaps)
   152→            return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
   153→        unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   154→        if unclustered:
   155→            return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   156→        # Advisory warnings (non-blocking but informational)
   157→        warnings: list[str] = []
   158→        overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
   159→        if overlaps:
   160→            warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
   161→        scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
   162→        if scattered:
   163→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _ in scattered)
   164→            warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
   165→        high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
   166→        if high_ratio:
   167→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
   168→            warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
   169→        # Orphaned clusters (steps but no issues)
   170→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   171→        orphaned = [
   172→            n for n, c in clusters.items()
   173→            if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
   174→        ]
   175→        if orphaned:
   176→            warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
   177→        if warnings:
   178→            # Return success with advisory message
   179→            return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
   180→        return True, ""
   181→
   182→    if stage == "enrich":
   183→        if "enrich" not in stages:
   184→            return False, "Enrich stage not recorded."
   185→        failures = run_enrich_quality_checks(plan, repo_root, phase_label="enrich")
   186→        if failures:
   187→            return False, failures[0].message
   188→        return True, ""
   189→
   190→    if stage == "sense-check":
   191→        if "sense-check" not in stages:
   192→            return False, "Sense-check stage not recorded."
   193→        report = stages["sense-check"].get("report", "")
   194→        if len(report) < 100:
   195→            return False, f"Sense-check report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   196→        failures = run_enrich_quality_checks(
   197→            plan,
   198→            repo_root,
   199→            phase_label="sense-check",
   200→        )
   201→        if failures:
   202→            return False, failures[0].message
   203→        return True, ""
   204→
   205→    return False, f"Unknown stage: {stage}"
   206→
   207→
   208→def validate_completion(
   209→    plan: dict,
   210→    state: dict,
   211→    repo_root: Path,
   212→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   213→    """Validate plan is ready for triage completion. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   214→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   215→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   216→
   217→    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"):
   218→        if required not in stages:
   219→            return False, f"Stage {required} not recorded."
   220→        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
   221→            return False, f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
   222→
   223→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   224→    if not manual:
   225→        return False, "No manual clusters with issues."
   226→
   227→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   228→    if gaps:
   229→        return False, f"{len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment."
   230→
   231→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   232→    if unclustered:
   233→        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   234→
   235→    # Quality advisories (non-blocking but surfaced to caller)
   236→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   237→    # Check dependency ordering
   238→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   239→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   240→        if name in deps:
   241→            return False, f"Cluster {name} depends on itself."
   242→
   243→    # Check for all-trivial clusters
   244→    all_trivial_clusters = []
   245→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   246→        if c.get("auto") or not c.get("issue_ids"):
   247→            continue
   248→        steps = c.get("action_steps") or []
   249→        if steps and all(
   250→            isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") == "trivial" for s in steps
   251→        ):
   252→            all_trivial_clusters.append(name)
   253→
   254→    if all_trivial_clusters:
   255→        names = ", ".join(sorted(all_trivial_clusters))
   256→        return True, f"Advisory: all action steps are marked trivial in cluster(s): {names}"
   257→
   258→    return True, ""
   259→
   260→
   261→def build_auto_attestation(
   262→    stage: str,
   263→    plan: dict,
   264→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   265→) -> str:
   266→    """Generate valid 80+ char attestation referencing real dimensions/cluster names."""
   267→    if stage == "observe":
   268→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   269→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   270→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   271→        return (
   272→            f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   273→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
   274→            f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
   275→        )
   276→
   277→    if stage == "reflect":
   278→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   279→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   280→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   281→        return (
   282→            f"My strategy accounts for {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   283→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, comparing against "
   284→            f"resolved history and forming priorities for execution."
   285→        )
   286→
   287→    if stage == "organize":
   288→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   289→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   290→        return (
   291→            f"I have organized all review issues into clusters including "
   292→            f"{names_str}, with descriptions, action steps, and clear "
   293→            f"priority ordering based on root cause analysis."
   294→        )
   295→
   296→    if stage == "enrich":
   297→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   298→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   299→        return (
   300→            f"Steps in clusters including {names_str} are executor-ready with "
   301→            f"detail, file paths, issue refs, and effort tags, verified "
   302→            f"against the actual codebase."
   303→        )
   304→
   305→    if stage == "sense-check":
   306→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   307→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   308→        return (
   309→            f"Content and structure verified for clusters including {names_str}. "
   310→            f"All step details are factually accurate, cross-cluster dependencies "
   311→            f"are safe, and enrich-level checks pass."
   312→        )
   313→
   314→    return f"Stage {stage} completed with thorough analysis of all available data and verified against codebase."
   315→
   316→
   317→__all__ = [
   318→    "EnrichQualityFailure",
   319→    "build_auto_attestation",
   320→    "run_enrich_quality_checks",
   321→    "validate_completion",
   322→    "validate_stage",
   323→]
   324→
```

```
1→"""Per-stage subagent prompt builders for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan import TriageInput, build_triage_prompt
    10→
    11→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    12→from .stage_prompts_instructions import (
    13→    _CLI_REFERENCE,
    14→    _PREAMBLE,
    15→    _STAGES,
    16→    _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS,
    17→)
    18→from .stage_prompts_observe import (
    19→    _observe_batch_instructions,
    20→    build_observe_batch_prompt,
    21→)
    22→from .stage_prompts_sense import (
    23→    build_sense_check_content_prompt,
    24→    build_sense_check_structure_prompt,
    25→)
    26→from .stage_prompts_validation import _validation_requirements
    27→
    28→
    29→def build_stage_prompt(
    30→    stage: str,
    31→    triage_input: TriageInput,
    32→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
    33→    *,
    34→    repo_root: Path,
    35→) -> str:
    36→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
    37→    parts: list[str] = []
    38→
    39→    # Preamble
    40→    parts.append(_PREAMBLE.format(stage=stage.upper(), repo_root=repo_root))
    41→
    42→    # Issue data
    43→    issue_data = build_triage_prompt(triage_input)
    44→    parts.append("## Issue Data\n\n" + issue_data)
    45→
    46→    # Prior stage reports
    47→    if prior_reports:
    48→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
    49→        for prior_stage, report in prior_reports.items():
    50→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
    51→
    52→    # Stage-specific instructions
    53→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
    54→    if instruction_fn:
    55→        parts.append(instruction_fn())
    56→
    57→    # CLI reference
    58→    parts.append(_CLI_REFERENCE)
    59→
    60→    # Validation requirements
    61→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
    62→
    63→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
    64→
    65→
    66→def cmd_stage_prompt(
    67→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    68→    *,
    69→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    70→) -> None:
    71→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
    72→    stage = args.stage_prompt
    73→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    74→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    75→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    76→    state = runtime.state
    77→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    78→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    79→
    80→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
    81→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    82→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    83→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
    84→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
    85→        if prior_stage == stage:
    86→            break
    87→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
    88→        if report:
    89→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
    90→
    91→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root)
    92→    print(prompt)
    93→
    94→
    95→__all__ = [
    96→    "build_observe_batch_prompt",
    97→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
    98→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
    99→    "build_stage_prompt",
   100→    "cmd_stage_prompt",
```

1→"""Shared static text blocks for triage stage prompts."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→_STAGES = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
     6→
     7→_PREAMBLE = """\
     8→You are a triage subagent with full codebase access and the desloppify CLI.
     9→Your job is to complete the **{stage}** stage of triage planning.
    10→
    11→Repo root: {repo_root}
    12→
    13→## Standards
    14→
    15→You are expected to produce **exceptional** work. The output of this triage becomes the
    16→actual plan that an executor follows — if you are lazy, vague, or sloppy, real work gets
    17→wasted. Concretely:
    18→
    19→- **Read the actual source code.** Every opinion you form must come from reading the file,
    20→  not from reading the issue title. Issues frequently exaggerate, miscount, or describe
    21→  code that has already been fixed. Trust nothing until you verify it.
    22→- **Have specific opinions.** "This seems like it could be an issue" is worthless. "This is
    23→  a false positive because line 47 already uses the pattern the issue suggests" is useful.
    24→- **Do the hard thinking.** If two issues seem related, figure out WHY. If something should
    25→  be skipped, explain the specific reason for THIS issue, not a generic category.
    26→- **Don't take shortcuts.** Reading 5 files and extrapolating to 30 is lazy. Read all 30.
    27→  If you have too many, use subagents to parallelize — don't skip.
    28→
    29→Use the desloppify CLI to record your work. Every command you run mutates plan.json directly.
    30→The orchestrator will review your work and confirm the stage after you record it.
    31→
    32→**CRITICAL: Only run commands for YOUR stage ({stage}).** Do NOT re-run earlier stages
    33→(e.g., do not run `--stage observe` if you are the organize subagent). Earlier stages
    34→are already confirmed. Re-running them will corrupt the plan state.
    35→"""
    36→
    37→_CLI_REFERENCE = """\
    38→## CLI Command Reference
    39→
    40→### Stage recording
    41→```
    42→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<analysis>"
    43→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<strategy>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    44→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    45→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    46→desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<verification summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    47→```
    48→
    49→### Cluster management
    50→```
    51→desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "<what this cluster addresses>"
    52→desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns...>
    53→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "<desc>" --steps "step 1" "step 2"
    54→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "<title>" --detail "<sub-points>" --effort small --issue-refs <id1> <id2>
    55→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "<sub-points>" --effort medium --issue-refs <id1>
    56→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other-cluster-name>
    57→desloppify plan cluster show <name>
    58→desloppify plan cluster list --verbose
    59→```
    60→
    61→### Skip/dismiss
    62→```
    63→desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason>" --attest "<attestation>"
    64→```
    65→
    66→### Effort tags
    67→Valid values: trivial, small, medium, large. Set on steps via --effort flag.
    68→"""
    69→
    70→
    71→__all__ = ["_CLI_REFERENCE", "_PREAMBLE", "_STAGES"]
    72→
